+"""Localhost web launcher for Aldervale.
+
+A small stdlib HTTP server serves a branded start page and a token-protected
+endpoint that opens the real game in a new terminal window. It also serves the
+inspection Studio from `.inspect/` so the launcher, Studio and game share one
+local web surface. No external dependency is used.
+"""
+
+import json
+import mimetypes
+import os
+import secrets
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import threading
+import time
+import urllib.error
+import urllib.request
+import webbrowser
+from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
+from pathlib import Path
+from urllib.parse import unquote, urlparse
+
+PROJECT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
+STUDIO_DIR = PROJECT / ".inspect"
+TOKEN = secrets.token_urlsafe(24)
+LAUNCHER_VERSION = "1.0"
+
+
+def game_command(mode):
+    """The fixed command line for a launch mode; never user supplied."""
+    cmd = [sys.executable, "-m", "aldervale"]
+    if mode == "demo":
+        cmd.extend(["--demo", "9"])
+    return cmd
+
+
+def spawn_game(mode):
+    """Open the real game in a new console window (Windows)."""
+    if mode not in ("play", "demo"):
+        return False, "unknown launch mode"
+    if os.name != "nt":
+        return False, ("Automatic launch is supported on Windows; run "
+                       "'python -m aldervale%s' manually." %
+                       (" --demo 9" if mode == "demo" else ""))
+    flags = getattr(subprocess, "CREATE_NEW_CONSOLE", 0)
+    try:
+        # hygiene: allow detached game process; waiting would block the launcher
+        subprocess.Popen(game_command(mode), cwd=str(PROJECT),
+                         creationflags=flags, close_fds=True)
+    except OSError as exc:
+        return False, "%s: %s" % (type(exc).__name__, exc)
+    return True, "Game launched in a new console window."
+
+
+def render_page(token):
+    return PAGE.replace("__TOKEN__", token)
+
+
+def ensure_web_session(server):
+    """Start building the browser-play session once, in the background."""
+    with server.web_lock:
+        if (server.web_session is not None or server.web_building or
+                server.web_error):
+            return
+        server.web_building = True
+
+    def build():
+        session = None
+        error = None
+        try:
+            from .webgame import WebGame
+            session = WebGame(seed=7, width=132, height=60)
+        except Exception as exc:
+            error = "%s: %s" % (type(exc).__name__, exc)
+        with server.web_lock:
+            server.web_session = session
+            server.web_building = False
+            server.web_error = error
+
+    threading.Thread(target=build, daemon=True).start()
+
+
+def launcher_url(port, autostart=False):
+    """The local URL, optionally asking the page to start the game on load."""
+    url = "http://127.0.0.1:%d/" % int(port)
+    return url + "?autostart=1" if autostart else url
+
+
+def play_url(port):
+    return launcher_url(port) + "play"
+
+
+def check_health(port):
+    """True when an Aldervale launcher already owns the port."""
+    if not port:
+        return False
+    try:
+        with urllib.request.urlopen(launcher_url(port) + "health",
+                                    timeout=0.5) as response:
+            return response.status == 200 and response.read(2) == b"ok"
+    except (OSError, urllib.error.URLError):
+        return False
+
+
+def _studio_file(name):
+    if not name or "/" in name or "\\" in name or name.startswith("."):
+        return None
+    candidate = STUDIO_DIR / name
+    return candidate if candidate.is_file() else None
+
+
+class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
+    server_version = "AldervaleLauncher/" + LAUNCHER_VERSION
+
+    def _send(self, code, body, ctype="text/html; charset=utf-8"):
+        if isinstance(body, str):
+            body = body.encode("utf-8")
+        self.send_response(code)
+        self.send_header("Content-Type", ctype)
+        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
+        self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-store")
+        self.send_header("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff")
+        self.send_header("Referrer-Policy", "no-referrer")
+        self.end_headers()
+        self.wfile.write(body)
+
+    def do_GET(self):
+        path = urlparse(self.path).path
+        if path in ("/", "/index.html"):
+            self._send(200, render_page(TOKEN))
+            return
+        if path == "/health":
+            self._send(200, "ok", "text/plain; charset=utf-8")
+            return
+        if path == "/favicon.ico":
+            self._send(204, b"", "image/x-icon")
+            return
+        if path == "/play":
+            from .webgame import PLAY_HTML
+            ensure_web_session(self.server)
+            self._send(200, PLAY_HTML.replace("__TOKEN__", TOKEN))
+            return
+        if path in ("/api/frame", "/api/state"):
+            server = self.server
+            session = server.web_session
+            if session is not None:
+                payload = (session.snapshot() if path == "/api/frame"
+                           else session.state())
+            elif server.web_error:
+                payload = {"status": "error", "message": server.web_error}
+            else:
+                payload = {"status": "loading"}
+            self._send(200, json.dumps(payload), "application/json")
+            return
+        if path == "/studio" or path == "/studio/":
+            path = "/studio/index.html"
+        if path.startswith("/studio/"):
+            name = unquote(path[len("/studio/"):])
+            target = _studio_file(name)
+            if target is None:
+                self._send(404, "Studio file not found. Run "
+                                "`python -m aldervale --inspect .inspect`.")
+                return
+            ctype = mimetypes.guess_type(name)[0] or "application/octet-stream"
+            self._send(200, target.read_bytes(), ctype)
+            return
+        self._send(404, "Not found")
+
+    def do_POST(self):
+        path = urlparse(self.path).path
+        if path not in ("/start", "/api/input"):
+            self._send(404, "Not found")
+            return
+        if self.headers.get("X-Aldervale-Token") != TOKEN:
+            self._send(403, json.dumps({"ok": False,
+                                        "message": "bad token"}),
+                       "application/json")
+            return
+        try:
+            length = min(8192, int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", "0")))
+            payload = json.loads(self.rfile.read(length) or b"{}")
+        except (ValueError, json.JSONDecodeError):
+            self._send(400, json.dumps({"ok": False,
+                                        "message": "bad request"}),
+                       "application/json")
+            return
+        if path == "/api/input":
+            session = self.server.web_session
+            if session is None:
+                self._send(409, json.dumps({"ok": False,
+                                            "message": "game is loading"}),
+                           "application/json")
+                return
+            session.set_input(payload.get("pressed"), payload.get("held"),
+                              payload.get("mouse"))
+            self._send(200, json.dumps({"ok": True}), "application/json")
+            return
+        mode = payload.get("mode", "play")
+        if mode not in ("play", "demo"):
+            self._send(400, json.dumps({"ok": False,
+                                        "message": "unknown mode"}),
+                       "application/json")
+            return
+        server = self.server
+        with server.launch_lock:
+            now = time.monotonic()
+            if (server.last_mode == mode and
+                    now - server.last_spawn < 2.0):
+                self._send(200, json.dumps(
+                    {"ok": True, "message": "Game is already launching."}),
+                    "application/json")
+                return
+            server.last_spawn = now
+            server.last_mode = mode
+        spawn = getattr(server, "spawn", spawn_game)
+        ok, message = spawn(mode)
+        self._send(200 if ok else 500,
+                   json.dumps({"ok": ok, "message": message}),
+                   "application/json")
+
+    def log_message(self, *args):
+        return
+
+
+def serve(port=8765, open_browser=False, autostart=False, open_play=False):
+    """Blocking launcher server; returns the exit code."""
+    target = play_url(port) if open_play else launcher_url(port, autostart)
+    if check_health(port):
+        print("launcher: already running at %s" % launcher_url(port))
+        if open_browser:
+            webbrowser.open_new_tab(target)
+        return 0
+    server = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", port), Handler)
+    server.daemon_threads = True
+    server.spawn = spawn_game
+    server.last_spawn = 0.0
+    server.last_mode = None
+    server.launch_lock = threading.Lock()
+    server.web_session = None
+    server.web_building = False
+    server.web_error = None
+    server.web_lock = threading.Lock()
+    actual = server.server_address[1]
+    target = play_url(actual) if open_play else launcher_url(actual, autostart)
+    print("launcher: %s  (Ctrl+C to stop)" % launcher_url(actual))
+    print("launcher: browser game at %s" % play_url(actual))
+    if open_browser:
+        webbrowser.open_new_tab(target)
+    try:
+        server.serve_forever()
+    except KeyboardInterrupt:
+        print("launcher: stopped")
+    finally:
+        server.shutdown()
+        session = getattr(server, "web_session", None)
+        if session is not None:
+            session.close()
+        server.server_close()
+    return 0
+
+
+PAGE = """<!doctype html>
+<html lang="en">
+<head>
+<meta charset="utf-8">
+<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1">
+<title>Aldervale Launcher</title>
+<style>
+:root{--bg:#0b0e14;--panel:#121722;--line:#283144;--ink:#dce5f2;
+      --dim:#8b97aa;--gold:#e8c45a;--green:#7fd18a;--red:#e07a7a}
+*{box-sizing:border-box}
+html,body{margin:0;min-height:100%;background:radial-gradient(circle at 50% 0,
+  #182238 0, #0b0e14 58%);color:var(--ink)}
+body{font-family:"Cascadia Mono",Consolas,"Courier New",monospace;
+     display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;padding:32px}
+main{width:min(760px,100%);background:rgba(14,19,29,.92);
+     border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:10px;padding:34px 38px;
+     box-shadow:0 24px 80px rgba(0,0,0,.55)}
+h1{margin:0;font-size:clamp(28px,6vw,54px);letter-spacing:.24em;
+   color:var(--gold);text-align:center}
+.sub{text-align:center;color:var(--dim);font-size:13px;margin:8px 0 30px}
+.buttons{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:12px;justify-content:center}
+button,.buttons a{border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:6px;
+       padding:14px 26px;background:#0e1420;color:var(--ink);font:inherit;
+       font-size:15px;cursor:pointer;letter-spacing:.08em;display:inline-block;
+       text-decoration:none}
+button.primary{border-color:var(--gold);color:var(--gold);
+               background:#161b26}
+.buttons a.web{border-color:var(--green);color:var(--green);
+               background:#161b26}
+button:hover,.buttons a:hover{filter:brightness(1.2)}
+button:disabled{opacity:.55;cursor:wait}
+#status{min-height:22px;text-align:center;margin:20px 0 6px;font-size:13px;
+        color:var(--dim)}
+#status.ok{color:var(--green)}#status.bad{color:var(--red)}
+.links{display:flex;gap:18px;justify-content:center;margin-top:16px;
+       font-size:13px}
+a{color:var(--gold);text-decoration:none}
+a:hover{text-decoration:underline}
+.help{margin-top:28px;border-top:1px solid var(--line);padding-top:18px;
+      color:var(--dim);font-size:12px;line-height:1.7}
+kbd{color:var(--ink);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:3px;
+    padding:1px 5px;font:inherit;font-size:11px}
+.note{text-align:center;color:#596477;font-size:11px;margin-top:18px}
+</style>
+</head>
+<body>
+<main>
+  <h1>ALDERVALE</h1>
+  <p class="sub">a tale of steel and starlight, told in ASCII cells</p>
+  <div class="buttons">
+    <button class="primary" data-mode="play">START GAME</button>
+    <button data-mode="demo">WATCH DEMO</button>
+    <a class="web" href="/play" target="_blank" rel="noopener">PLAY IN BROWSER</a>
+  </div>
+  <p id="status">Ready. Starting opens the game in a new terminal window.</p>
+  <div class="links">
+    <a href="/studio/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open Studio</a>
+    <a href="/health" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Health</a>
+  </div>
+  <div class="help">
+    Controls in the game window: <kbd>WASD</kbd> walk,
+    <kbd>SPACE</kbd> attack, <kbd>E</kbd> talk, <kbd>I</kbd> bag,
+    <kbd>M</kbd> map, <kbd>H</kbd> help, <kbd>ESC</kbd> pause.
+    The Studio opens the exact browser-rendered frames and the model
+    turnaround viewer.
+  </div>
+  <p class="note">Launcher v__VERSION__ . localhost only . token-protected</p>
+</main>
+<script>
+const TOKEN = "__TOKEN__";
+const status = document.getElementById("status");
+
+async function launch(mode) {
+  const buttons = [...document.querySelectorAll("button[data-mode]")];
+  buttons.forEach(b => b.disabled = true);
+  status.className = "";
+  status.textContent = mode === "demo"
+    ? "Opening the demo..." : "Opening the game...";
+  try {
+    const r = await fetch("/start", {
+      method: "POST",
+      headers: {"Content-Type": "application/json",
+                "X-Aldervale-Token": TOKEN},
+      body: JSON.stringify({mode})
+    });
+    const data = await r.json();
+    status.className = data.ok ? "ok" : "bad";
+    status.textContent = data.message;
+  } catch (err) {
+    status.className = "bad";
+    status.textContent = "Launcher error: " + err;
+  } finally {
+    buttons.forEach(b => b.disabled = false);
+  }
+}
+
+document.querySelectorAll("button[data-mode]").forEach(btn => {
+  btn.addEventListener("click", () => launch(btn.dataset.mode));
+});
+
+const params = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
+if (params.get("autostart") === "1") {
+  history.replaceState(null, "", location.pathname);
+  if (!sessionStorage.getItem("aldervale_autostart")) {
+    sessionStorage.setItem("aldervale_autostart", "1");
+    setTimeout(() => launch("play"), 350);
+  }
+}
+</script>
+</body>
+</html>
+""".replace("__VERSION__", LAUNCHER_VERSION)

+def _color_delta(c1, c2):
+    return (abs(((c1 >> 16) & 255) - ((c2 >> 16) & 255)) +
+            abs(((c1 >> 8) & 255) - ((c2 >> 8) & 255)) +
+            abs((c1 & 255) - (c2 & 255)))
+
+
+def _inner_edges(chars, colors, fids, fshade, cw, ch):
+    """Line art only on meaningful face boundaries, never on flat facets."""
+    marks = {}
+    for y in range(ch):
+        row = y * cw
+        up = row - cw
+        for x in range(cw):
+            idx = row + x
+            if chars[idx] == " ":
+                continue
+            fid = fids[idx]
+            vert = False
+            horiz = False
+            if x > 0 and fids[idx - 1] not in (-1, fid):
+                if _color_delta(colors[idx], colors[idx - 1]) >= 58:
+                    vert = True
+            if y > 0 and fids[up + x] not in (-1, fid):
+                if _color_delta(colors[idx], colors[up + x]) >= 58:
+                    horiz = True
+            if vert or horiz:
+                marks[idx] = "+" if (vert and horiz) else ("|" if vert else "-")
+    keep = {}
+    for idx, ch in marks.items():
+        x = idx % cw
+        if ch in ("|", "+"):
+            if ((idx - cw) in marks and marks[idx - cw] in ("|", "+")) or \
+               ((idx + cw) in marks and marks[idx + cw] in ("|", "+")):
+                keep[idx] = ch
+                continue
+        if ch in ("-", "+"):
+            if ((x > 0 and (idx - 1) in marks and marks[idx - 1] in ("-", "+"))
+                    or (x + 1 < cw and (idx + 1) in marks
+                        and marks[idx + 1] in ("-", "+"))):
+                keep[idx] = ch
+    for idx, ch in keep.items():
+        c = colors[idx]
+        chars[idx] = ch
+        colors[idx] = pack_rgb(((c >> 16) & 255) * 0.80,
+                               ((c >> 8) & 255) * 0.80,
+                               (c & 255) * 0.80)
+

+def clear_camera_distance(terrain, px, pz, eye_y, fx, fz, cam_y, desired):
+    """Pull the camera in when a hill would sit between it and the player."""
+    steps = 9
+    for i in range(1, steps + 1):
+        t = i / steps
+        sx = px - fx * desired * t
+        sz = pz - fz * desired * t
+        gh = terrain.height(sx, sz)
+        line = eye_y + (cam_y - eye_y) * t
+        if gh > line - 0.15:
+            return max(2.2, desired * t - 0.5)
+    return desired
+
+

     """View parameters plus precomputed per-column ray angles."""

+def _weather(screen, weather, env):
+    """Deterministic rain streaks or drifting snow, drawn above the world."""
+    if weather not in ("rain", "snow"):
+        return
+    w, h = screen.w, screen.h
+    t = env.t
+    if weather == "rain":
+        color = pack_rgb(146, 176, 212)
+        drops = max(16, (w * h) // 220)
+        for i in range(drops):
+            x = int(rand01(i, 7, 31) * w + t * 26.0 + i * 3) % w
+            y = int(rand01(i, 11, 37) * h + t * 62.0 + i * 5) % h
+            screen.set(x, y, "/" if i & 1 else "\\", color, 0.0)
+            if y > 0:
+                screen.set(x, y - 1, "|", color, 0.0)
+    else:
+        color = pack_rgb(226, 236, 248)
+        flakes = max(18, (w * h) // 260)
+        for i in range(flakes):
+            x = int(rand01(i, 5, 41) * w +
+                    math.sin(t * 0.8 + i) * 2.2 + i * 1.7) % w
+            y = int(rand01(i, 9, 43) * h + t * 7.0 + i * 2.3) % h
+            screen.set(x, y, "*" if i % 9 == 0 else '"', color, 0.0)
+

+def _birds(screen, cam, env):
+    """Three birds cross the day sky, flapping on a slow rhythm."""
+    if env.daylight < 0.35 or cam.horizon < 8:
+        return
+    soft = pack_rgb(58, 66, 84)
+    for i in range(3):
+        az = (0.7 + i * 1.9 + env.t * (0.016 + i * 0.004)) % 6.28318
+        rel = az - cam.yaw
+        while rel > math.pi:
+            rel -= 6.28318
+        while rel < -math.pi:
+            rel += 6.28318
+        if abs(rel) > FOV * 0.55:
+            continue
+        el = 0.62 + 0.10 * math.sin(env.t * 0.2 + i * 1.7)
+        col = int(screen.w * 0.5 +
+                  math.tan(rel) / math.tan(FOV * 0.5) * screen.w * 0.5)
+        row = int(cam.horizon - el * cam.fy)
+        if 0 <= col < screen.w and 0 <= row < cam.horizon:
+            flap = math.sin(env.t * 2.6 + i * 2.1)
+            screen.set(col, row, "v" if flap > 0.0 else "^", soft, FAR)
+
+
+def _mountains(screen, cam, env):
+    """Three fogged ridge layers above the horizon, each with a broken edge."""
+    ridges = _ridges()
+    w = screen.w
+    horizon = cam.horizon
+    if horizon < 6:
+        return
+    layers = ((0.24, 0.16, " ."), (0.17, 0.24, " :"),
+              (0.11, 0.34, ".:"))
+    for li, (amp, dark, edge) in enumerate(layers):
+        vals = ridges[li]
+        base = _mix(env.horizon_col, RIDGE_COLOR, dark)
+        body = _mix(base, RIDGE_COLOR, 0.35)
+        shade = _mix(body, RIDGE_COLOR, 0.35)
+        for x in range(w):
+            az = cam.yaw + cam.col_angle[x] + math.pi
+            idx = int((az % 6.28318) / 6.28318 * 767) & 767
+            r = vals[idx]
+            peak = int(r * horizon * amp)
+            if peak < 1:
+                continue
+            top = horizon - peak
+            prev = vals[(idx - 1) & 767]
+            slope = r - prev
+            for y in range(max(0, top), horizon):
+                hsh = (x * 1103515245 + y * 12345) & 7
+                if y == top:
+                    if slope > 0.012 and hsh < 2:
+                        ch = "/"
+                    elif slope < -0.012 and hsh < 2:
+                        ch = "\\"
+                    elif hsh == 0:
+                        ch = "^"
+                    else:
+                        continue
+                    col = base
+                elif hsh < 6:
+                    ch = edge[1] if y < top + 3 else (
+                        ":" if hsh < 4 else ("." if hsh < 5 else ";"))
+                    col = body if ((x + y) & 3) else shade
+                else:
+                    continue
+                screen.chars[y][x] = ch
+                screen.fg[y][x] = col
+                screen.depth[y][x] = FAR

+def _light_pools(screen, world, cam, env):
+    """Warm stippled pools under campfires and shrines, strongest at night."""
+    if screen.w < 8 or screen.h < 8:
+        return
+    sin_y = math.sin(cam.yaw)
+    cos_y = math.cos(cam.yaw)
+    night = 0.25 + 0.75 * max(0.0, env.night)
+    for ent in world.entities:
+        is_house = ent.model.startswith("house")
+        if ent.model not in ("campfire", "shrine", "torch") and not is_house:
+            continue
+        lx, lz, ly = ent.x, ent.z, ent.ground_y
+        if is_house:
+            lx = ent.x + math.sin(ent.yaw) * 2.05
+            lz = ent.z + math.cos(ent.yaw) * 2.05
+            ly = world.terrain.height(lx, lz)
+        dx = lx - cam.x
+        dz = lz - cam.z
+        depth = dx * sin_y + dz * cos_y
+        if depth < 1.5 or depth > 46.0:
+            continue
+        side = dx * cos_y - dz * sin_y
+        col = cam.w * 0.5 + side / depth * cam.f
+        row = cam.horizon - (ly - cam.y) / depth * cam.fy
+        if ent.model == "torch":
+            radius = max(3, min(12, int(2.0 / depth * cam.f)))
+        elif is_house:
+            radius = max(2, min(9, int(1.5 / depth * cam.f)))
+        else:
+            radius = max(3, min(14, int(2.2 / depth * cam.f)))
+        if col < -radius or col > screen.w + radius:
+            continue
+        col = int(col)
+        row = int(row)
+        for py in range(max(0, row - radius),
+                        min(screen.h, row + radius + 1)):
+            for px in range(max(0, col - radius),
+                            min(screen.w, col + radius + 1)):
+                if screen.depth[py][px] >= FAR:
+                    continue
+                ex = (px - col) / radius
+                ey = (py - row) / max(1.0, radius * 0.5)
+                d = ex * ex + ey * ey
+                if d >= 1.0:
+                    continue
+                k = (1.0 - d) ** 1.8 * night
+                if k <= 0.01:
+                    continue
+                if d > 0.28 and ((px * 11 + py * 7) & 15) > k * 14.0:
+                    continue

+                    strength = 0.82
+                elif is_house:
+                    strength = 0.48
+                else:
+                    strength = 0.62
+                screen.fg[py][px] = _mix(screen.fg[py][px], FIRE_COLOR,
+                                         min(strength, k * 0.8))
+

+def _smoke(screen, world, cam, env):
+    """Thin chimney smoke above village roofs, drifting with the wind."""

+        return
+    sin_y = math.sin(cam.yaw)

+            row = int(cam.horizon - (wy - cam.y) / depth * cam.fy)
+            if not (0 <= col < screen.w and 0 <= row < cam.horizon):

@@ -0,0 +1,490 @@
+"""Browser studio: render the exact char-cell frame with a real terminal font.
+
+The PNG capture uses a small built-in bitmap font and tinted cell backgrounds,
+which is convenient but not what the game actually looks like.  This module
+writes a standalone HTML page and a JSON payload of raw cells, so a browser (or
+headless Edge/Chrome) shows the same grid the terminal receives, and a sprite
+gallery can be inspected at any zoom.
+
+No third-party Python dependency is used.  Headless screenshots are an optional
+convenience that shells out to a browser the user already has installed.
+"""
+
+import json
+import os
+import shutil
+import subprocess
+import tempfile
+import time
+from pathlib import Path
+
+STUDIO_MODELS = (
+    ("knight", ("idle", "walk1", "atk1")),
+    ("guard", ("idle",)),
+    ("skeleton", ("idle", "atk")),
+    ("captain", ("idle", "atk")),
+    ("wolf", ("idle", "atk")),
+    ("bandit", ("idle", "atk")),
+    ("elder", ("idle",)),
+    ("smith", ("idle",)),
+)
+YAW_SAMPLES = 8
+
+
+def encode_screen(screen):
+    """Raw cells and packed colours for one rendered frame."""
+    return {
+        "w": screen.w,
+        "h": screen.h,
+        "chars": ["".join(row) for row in screen.chars],
+        "colors": [[int(c) for c in row] for row in screen.fg],
+    }
+
+
+def encode_sprite(sprite):
+    """Raw cells and packed colours for one baked sprite."""
+    return {
+        "w": sprite.w,
+        "h": sprite.h,
+        "chars": ["".join(row) for row in sprite.chars],
+        "colors": [[int(c) for c in row] for row in sprite.colors],
+    }
+
+
+def collect_models(atlas):
+    """Sample every studio model/pose at evenly spaced yaw frames."""
+    yaw_steps = max(1, int(getattr(atlas, "yaw_steps", 20)))
+    yaw_indices = sorted({int(i * yaw_steps // YAW_SAMPLES) % yaw_steps
+                          for i in range(YAW_SAMPLES)})
+    entries = {}
+    for model, poses in STUDIO_MODELS:
+        pose_data = {}
+        for pose in poses:
+            frames = []
+            for idx in yaw_indices:
+                sprite = atlas.get(model, pose, idx)
+                frames.append(encode_sprite(sprite) if sprite is not None
+                              else None)
+            if any(frames):
+                pose_data[pose] = frames
+        if pose_data:
+            entries[model] = pose_data
+    return {"yaw_steps": yaw_steps, "yaw_indices": yaw_indices,
+            "entries": entries}
+
+
+def write_studio(outdir, seed, viewport, scenes, models, checks):
+    """Write index.html + studio-data.js; return their paths."""
+    out = Path(outdir)
+    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    data = {"generated": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
+            "seed": seed, "viewport": list(viewport),
+            "scenes": scenes, "models": models, "checks": checks}
+    blob = json.dumps(data, separators=(",", ":")).replace("</", "<\\/")
+    data_path = out / "studio-data.js"
+    data_path.write_text("window.STUDIO = " + blob + ";\n", encoding="utf-8")
+    html_path = out / "index.html"
+    html_path.write_text(STUDIO_HTML, encoding="utf-8")
+    return str(html_path), str(data_path)
+
+
+def find_browser():
+    """First installed Chromium-family browser, or None."""
+    override = os.environ.get("ALDERVALE_BROWSER")
+    if override and Path(override).is_file():
+        return override
+    for name in ("msedge", "chrome", "chromium", "google-chrome",
+                 "chromium-browser"):
+        found = shutil.which(name)
+        if found:
+            return found
+    roots = [os.environ.get("ProgramFiles(x86)"),
+             os.environ.get("ProgramFiles"),
+             os.environ.get("LOCALAPPDATA")]
+    rel = (r"Microsoft\Edge\Application\msedge.exe",
+           r"Google\Chrome\Application\chrome.exe")
+    for root in roots:
+        if not root:
+            continue
+        for tail in rel:
+            candidate = Path(root) / tail
+            if candidate.is_file():
+                return str(candidate)
+    for candidate in ("/usr/bin/chromium", "/usr/bin/chromium-browser",
+                      "/usr/bin/google-chrome",
+                      "/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome",
+                      "/Applications/Microsoft Edge.app/Contents/MacOS/Microsoft Edge"):
+        if Path(candidate).is_file():
+            return candidate
+    return None
+
+
+def screenshot_studio(html_path, out_path, query="", size=(1600, 1000)):
+    """Screenshot a studio page with headless Edge/Chrome; return (ok, detail)."""
+    browser = find_browser()
+    if browser is None:
+        return False, "no Edge/Chrome found"
+    html = Path(html_path).resolve()
+    if not html.is_file():
+        return False, "studio page missing: %s" % html
+    out = Path(out_path).resolve()
+    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    url = html.as_uri()
+    if query:
+        url += "?" + query.lstrip("?")
+    width = max(320, int(size[0]))
+    height = max(240, int(size[1]))
+    profile = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="aldervale-web-")
+    try:
+        base = [browser, "--disable-gpu", "--hide-scrollbars",
+                "--no-first-run", "--no-default-browser-check",
+                "--virtual-time-budget=2500",
+                "--window-size=%d,%d" % (width, height),
+                "--user-data-dir=" + profile,
+                "--screenshot=" + str(out), url]
+        for mode in ("--headless=new", "--headless"):
+            result = subprocess.run([browser, mode] + base[1:],
+                                    stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,
+                                    stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
+                                    timeout=90, check=False)
+            if out.is_file() and out.stat().st_size > 0:
+                return True, "%s (%d bytes)" % (out, out.stat().st_size)
+            if result.returncode == 0:
+                break
+        return False, "browser produced no screenshot"
+    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as exc:
+        return False, "%s: %s" % (type(exc).__name__, exc)
+    finally:
+        shutil.rmtree(profile, ignore_errors=True)
+
+
+STUDIO_HTML = """<!doctype html>
+<html lang="en">
+<head>
+<meta charset="utf-8">
+<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1">
+<title>Aldervale Studio</title>
+<style>
+:root{--bg:#0b0e14;--panel:#121722;--line:#232b39;--ink:#dce5f2;
+      --dim:#8b97aa;--gold:#e8c45a;--green:#7fd18a;--red:#e07a7a}
+*{box-sizing:border-box}
+html,body{margin:0;background:var(--bg);color:var(--ink)}
+body{font-family:"Cascadia Mono",Consolas,"Courier New",monospace}
+header{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;gap:16px;
+       padding:12px 16px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);
+       background:var(--panel)}
+h1{font-size:16px;letter-spacing:.18em;margin:0;color:var(--gold)}
+.sub{color:var(--dim);font-size:11px}
+.tabs{display:flex;gap:6px;margin-left:auto}
+.tabs button,.controls button,.controls select,.controls input{
+  background:#0e1420;color:var(--ink);border:1px solid var(--line);
+  border-radius:4px;padding:5px 9px;font:inherit;font-size:12px}
+.tabs button.on{border-color:var(--gold);color:var(--gold)}
+main{padding:14px 16px 24px}
+.panel{display:none}
+.panel.on{display:block}
+.controls{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:10px;align-items:center;
+          margin-bottom:12px;font-size:12px;color:var(--dim)}
+.controls label{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px}
+.frame-host{overflow:auto;background:#000;border:1px solid var(--line);
+            padding:14px;max-height:78vh}
+.frame-host.dark{background:#05070a}
+.frame-pre{margin:0;line-height:1;letter-spacing:0;white-space:pre;
+           font-family:"Cascadia Mono",Consolas,"Courier New",monospace}
+.frame-pre span{white-space:pre}
+.caption{margin-top:8px;color:var(--dim);font-size:12px}
+table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%;font-size:12px}
+th,td{border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);padding:7px 8px;text-align:left}
+th{color:var(--gold);font-weight:normal}
+.ok{color:var(--green)}.bad{color:var(--red)}
+body.shot{background:#000}
+body.shot header,body.shot .controls{display:none}
+body.shot main{padding:0}
+body.shot .panel{display:none}
+body.shot .panel.on{display:flex;min-height:100vh;align-items:center;
+                    justify-content:center}
+body.shot .frame-host{border:0;padding:0;max-height:none;background:#000;
+                      overflow:hidden}
+body.shot .caption{position:fixed;left:10px;bottom:6px;margin:0;
+                   font-size:11px;color:#4f5968}
+</style>
+</head>
+<body>
+<header>
+  <h1>ALDERVALE STUDIO</h1>
+  <span class="sub" id="subtitle"></span>
+  <nav class="tabs">
+    <button data-tab="scenes" class="on">Scenes</button>
+    <button data-tab="models">Models</button>
+    <button data-tab="checks">Checks</button>
+  </nav>
+</header>
+<main>
+  <section class="panel on" id="panel-scenes">
+    <div class="controls">
+      <label>Scene <select id="sceneSelect"></select></label>
+      <label>Scale <input id="sceneScale" type="range" min="0.5" max="3"
+             step="0.25" value="1"></label>
+      <label><input id="sceneFit" type="checkbox"> fit window</label>
+      <span id="sceneInfo"></span>
+    </div>
+    <div class="frame-host" id="sceneHost"><pre class="frame-pre"></pre></div>
+    <div class="caption" id="sceneCaption"></div>
+  </section>
+  <section class="panel" id="panel-models">
+    <div class="controls">
+      <label>Model <select id="modelSelect"></select></label>
+      <label>Pose <select id="poseSelect"></select></label>
+      <label>Yaw <select id="yawSelect"></select></label>
+      <label>Scale <input id="modelScale" type="range" min="1" max="8"
+             step="0.5" value="4"></label>
+      <label><input id="modelFit" type="checkbox"> fit window</label>
+      <label><input id="modelGrid" type="checkbox"> grid</label>
+      <label><input id="modelSpin" type="checkbox"> spin</label>
+      <label><input id="modelTurn" type="checkbox"> all yaws</label>
+      <span id="modelInfo"></span>
+    </div>
+    <div class="frame-host" id="modelHost"><pre class="frame-pre"></pre></div>
+    <div class="caption" id="modelCaption"></div>
+  </section>
+  <section class="panel" id="panel-checks">
+    <table><thead><tr><th>Check</th><th>Result</th><th>Detail</th></tr></thead>
+    <tbody id="checkBody"></tbody></table>
+  </section>
+</main>
+<script src="studio-data.js"></script>
+<script>
+const S = window.STUDIO;
+const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
+const params = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
+let current = {scene: 0, model: 0, pose: 0, yaw: 0};
+let spinTimer = null;
+
+function hex(c){ return "#" + c.toString(16).padStart(6, "0"); }
+
+function renderFrame(host, frame, scale, grid){
+  host.replaceChildren();
+  const pre = document.createElement("pre");
+  pre.className = "frame-pre";
+  pre.style.fontSize = (8 * scale) + "px";
+  if (grid){
+    pre.style.backgroundImage =
+      "linear-gradient(#1a2230 1px, transparent 1px)," +
+      "linear-gradient(90deg, #1a2230 1px, transparent 1px)";
+    pre.style.backgroundSize = (8 * scale) + "px " + (8 * scale) + "px";
+  }
+  for (let y = 0; y < frame.h; y++){
+    const chars = frame.chars[y], colors = frame.colors[y];
+    let start = 0, col = colors[0];
+    for (let x = 1; x <= frame.w; x++){
+      if (x === frame.w || colors[x] !== col){
+        const span = document.createElement("span");
+        span.textContent = chars.slice(start, x);
+        span.style.color = hex(col);
+        pre.appendChild(span);
+        if (x < frame.w){ start = x; col = colors[x]; }
+      }
+    }
+    if (y < frame.h - 1) pre.appendChild(document.createTextNode("\\n"));
+  }
+  host.appendChild(pre);
+}
+
+function fitScale(frame, maxW, maxH){
+  const cellW = 4.8, cellH = 8;
+  return Math.max(0.5, Math.min(maxW / (frame.w * cellW),
+                                maxH / (frame.h * cellH)));
+}
+
+function sceneFrame(){ return S.scenes[current.scene].frame; }
+function modelFrame(){
+  const entry = S.models.entries[$("modelSelect").value];
+  const pose = $("poseSelect").value;
+  return {frame: entry[pose][current.yaw],
+          label: $("modelSelect").value + "  " + pose +
+                 "  yaw#" + S.models.yaw_indices[current.yaw]};
+}
+
+function drawScene(){
+  const meta = S.scenes[current.scene];
+  const fit = $("sceneFit").checked;
+  const scale = fit ? fitScale(meta.frame, innerWidth - 40,
+                               innerHeight - 120)
+                    : parseFloat($("sceneScale").value);
+  renderFrame($("sceneHost"), meta.frame, scale, false);
+  $("sceneInfo").textContent = meta.frame.w + "x" + meta.frame.h +
+    "  scale " + scale.toFixed(2);
+  $("sceneCaption").textContent =
+    "scene " + meta.name + "  .  day " + Number(meta.day_t).toFixed(2);
+}
+
+function drawTurnaround(){
+  const host = $("modelHost");
+  host.replaceChildren();
+  const entry = S.models.entries[$("modelSelect").value];
+  const pose = $("poseSelect").value;
+  const scale = parseFloat($("modelScale").value);
+  const wrap = document.createElement("div");
+  wrap.style.display = "grid";
+  wrap.style.gridTemplateColumns = "repeat(4, max-content)";
+  wrap.style.gap = "14px 18px";
+  wrap.style.alignItems = "flex-start";
+  S.models.yaw_indices.forEach((yaw, i) => {
+    const cell = document.createElement("div");
+    const cap = document.createElement("div");
+    cap.className = "sub";
+    cap.textContent = "yaw " + yaw;
+    cap.style.marginBottom = "4px";
+    const inner = document.createElement("div");
+    inner.className = "frame-host";
+    inner.style.padding = "4px";
+    renderFrame(inner, entry[pose][i], scale, $("modelGrid").checked);
+    cell.appendChild(cap);
+    cell.appendChild(inner);
+    wrap.appendChild(cell);
+  });
+  host.appendChild(wrap);
+  $("modelInfo").textContent = S.models.yaw_indices.length +
+    " yaws  " + entry[pose][0].w + "x" + entry[pose][0].h +
+    "  scale " + scale.toFixed(2);
+  $("modelCaption").textContent = $("modelSelect").value + "  " + pose +
+    "  turnaround";
+}
+
+function drawModel(){
+  if ($("modelTurn").checked){ drawTurnaround(); return; }
+  const picked = modelFrame();
+  if (!picked.frame) return;
+  const fit = $("modelFit").checked;
+  const scale = fit ? fitScale(picked.frame, innerWidth - 40,
+                               innerHeight - 120)
+                    : parseFloat($("modelScale").value);
+  renderFrame($("modelHost"), picked.frame, scale, $("modelGrid").checked);
+  $("modelInfo").textContent = picked.frame.w + "x" + picked.frame.h +
+    "  scale " + scale.toFixed(2);
+  $("modelCaption").textContent = picked.label;
+}
+
+function setSpin(on){
+  if (spinTimer){ clearInterval(spinTimer); spinTimer = null; }
+  if (!on) return;
+  spinTimer = setInterval(() => {
+    if (!S.models.yaw_indices.length) return;
+    current.yaw = (current.yaw + 1) % S.models.yaw_indices.length;
+    $("yawSelect").value = String(current.yaw);
+    drawModel();
+  }, 200);
+}
+
+function refreshPoses(){
+  const entry = S.models.entries[$("modelSelect").value];
+  $("poseSelect").replaceChildren(...Object.keys(entry).map(p => {
+    const o = document.createElement("option"); o.value = p;
+    o.textContent = p; return o;
+  }));
+  current.pose = 0;
+  refreshYaws();
+}
+function refreshYaws(){
+  $("yawSelect").replaceChildren(...S.models.yaw_indices.map((v, i) => {
+    const o = document.createElement("option"); o.value = String(i);
+    o.textContent = "yaw " + v; return o;
+  }));
+  current.yaw = 0;
+}
+
+function setTab(name){
+  document.querySelectorAll(".tabs button").forEach(b =>
+    b.classList.toggle("on", b.dataset.tab === name));
+  document.querySelectorAll(".panel").forEach(p =>
+    p.classList.toggle("on", p.id === "panel-" + name));
+  if (name === "scenes") drawScene();
+  if (name === "models") drawModel();
+}
+
+document.querySelectorAll(".tabs button").forEach(b =>
+  b.addEventListener("click", () => setTab(b.dataset.tab)));
+
+function init(){
+  $("subtitle").textContent = "seed " + S.seed + " . " +
+    S.viewport[0] + "x" + S.viewport[1] + " . " + S.generated;
+  $("sceneSelect").replaceChildren(...S.scenes.map((s, i) => {
+    const o = document.createElement("option"); o.value = String(i);
+    o.textContent = s.name; return o;
+  }));
+  $("modelSelect").replaceChildren(...Object.keys(S.models.entries).map(m => {
+    const o = document.createElement("option"); o.value = m;
+    o.textContent = m; return o;
+  }));
+  const tbody = $("checkBody");
+  S.checks.forEach(c => {
+    const tr = document.createElement("tr");
+    [c.name, c.ok ? "PASS" : "FAIL", c.detail].forEach((v, i) => {
+      const td = document.createElement("td");
+      td.textContent = v;
+      if (i === 1) td.className = c.ok ? "ok" : "bad";
+      tr.appendChild(td);
+    });
+    tbody.appendChild(tr);
+  });
+
+  const sceneName = params.get("scene");
+  if (sceneName){
+    const idx = S.scenes.findIndex(s => s.name === sceneName);
+    if (idx >= 0) current.scene = idx;
+  }
+  $("sceneSelect").value = String(current.scene);
+  if (params.get("sceneScale")) $("sceneScale").value = params.get("sceneScale");
+  if (params.get("sceneFit") === "1") $("sceneFit").checked = true;
+
+  const modelName = params.get("model");
+  if (modelName && S.models.entries[modelName])
+    $("modelSelect").value = modelName;
+  refreshPoses();
+  const pose = params.get("pose");
+  if (pose && [...$("poseSelect").options].some(o => o.value === pose))
+    $("poseSelect").value = pose;
+  const yaw = parseInt(params.get("yaw") || "0", 10);
+  if (yaw >= 0 && yaw < S.models.yaw_indices.length) current.yaw = yaw;
+  $("yawSelect").value = String(current.yaw);
+  if (params.get("scale")) $("modelScale").value = params.get("scale");
+  if (params.get("fit") === "1") $("modelFit").checked = true;
+  if (params.get("grid") === "1") $("modelGrid").checked = true;
+  if (params.get("spin") === "1") $("modelSpin").checked = true;
+  if (params.get("turn") === "1") $("modelTurn").checked = true;
+
+  $("sceneSelect").addEventListener("change", e => {
+    current.scene = parseInt(e.target.value, 10); drawScene();
+  });
+  $("sceneScale").addEventListener("input", drawScene);
+  $("sceneFit").addEventListener("change", drawScene);
+  $("modelSelect").addEventListener("change", () => { refreshPoses(); drawModel(); });
+  $("poseSelect").addEventListener("change", () => { current.pose = 0; drawModel(); });
+  $("yawSelect").addEventListener("change", e => {
+    current.yaw = parseInt(e.target.value, 10); drawModel();
+  });
+  $("modelScale").addEventListener("input", drawModel);
+  $("modelFit").addEventListener("change", drawModel);
+  $("modelGrid").addEventListener("change", drawModel);
+  $("modelSpin").addEventListener("change", e => setSpin(e.target.checked));
+  $("modelTurn").addEventListener("change", drawModel);
+
+  if (params.get("shot") === "1") document.body.classList.add("shot");
+  setSpin($("modelSpin").checked);
+  const tab = params.get("tab") || "scenes";
+  setTab(tab === "models" ? "models" : (tab === "checks" ? "checks" : "scenes"));
+  if (document.body.classList.contains("shot")){
+    if (tab === "models"){
+      $("modelFit").checked = true;
+      drawModel();
+    } else if (tab === "scenes"){
+      $("sceneFit").checked = true;
+      drawScene();
+    }
+  }
+}
+init();
+</script>
+</body>
+</html>

@@ -0,0 +1,464 @@
+"""Browser play client: a localhost thin client over the real Python engine.
+
+The browser sends key/mouse state and draws a canvas from char/colour frames
+produced by the existing Game. The terminal build is unchanged; this module is
+only the web transport and the canvas renderer. No external dependency is used.
+"""
+
+import json
+import threading
+import time
+from pathlib import Path
+
+from .inspect import build_game
+
+DT = 1.0 / 20.0
+VALID_TOKENS = frozenset((
+    "w", "a", "s", "d", "up", "down", "left", "right", "space", "shift",
+    "b", "e", "i", "m", "h", "esc", "enter", "tab", "f1", "+", "=", "-",
+    "q", "c", "n", "x", "1", "2", "3", "4", "5", "6", "7", "8", "9",
+))
+MOUSE_KINDS = frozenset(("ldown", "rdown", "up", "move", "wheel"))
+MAX_QUEUED_MOUSE = 64
+
+
+def encode_frame(screen):
+    """Rows of glyphs plus packed RGB colours for the canvas client."""
+    return {"chars": ["".join(row) for row in screen.chars],
+            "colors": [[int(c) for c in row] for row in screen.fg]}
+
+
+class WebTerm:
+    """Terminal double used by Game; queues input and ignores output."""
+
+    def __init__(self, width, height):
+        self.width = width
+        self.height = height
+        self.pressed = []
+        self.held = set()
+        self.mouse = []
+
+    def size(self):
+        return self.width, self.height
+
+    def poll(self):
+        pressed = self.pressed
+        self.pressed = []
+        return pressed, set(self.held)
+
+    def take_mouse(self):
+        events = self.mouse
+        self.mouse = []
+        return events
+
+    def write(self, text):
+        return None
+
+    def flush(self):
+        return None
+
+
+def _clean_mouse(events):
+    cleaned = []
+    for ev in events[:8]:
+        if not isinstance(ev, list) or len(ev) != 5:
+            continue
+        kind, col, row, buttons, wheel = ev
+        if kind not in MOUSE_KINDS:
+            continue
+        try:
+            col = max(-2, min(4000, int(col)))
+            row = max(-2, min(4000, int(row)))
+            buttons = int(buttons)
+            wheel = int(wheel)
+        except (TypeError, ValueError):
+            continue
+        cleaned.append((kind, col, row, buttons, wheel))
+    return cleaned
+
+
+class WebGame:
+    """One live session: Game + WebTerm + a 20 fps simulation thread."""
+
+    def __init__(self, seed=7, width=132, height=60):
+        self.width = width
+        self.height = height
+        self.game = build_game(seed=seed, width=width, height=height)
+        self.game.save_path = str(
+            Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "saves" /
+            "aldervale_web.json")
+        self.term = WebTerm(width, height)
+        self.game.term = self.term
+        self.game.state = "play"
+        self.game.fps = int(round(1.0 / DT))
+        self.game.dt = DT
+        self.lock = threading.RLock()
+        self.running = True
+        self.seq = 0
+        self.frame = encode_frame(self.game.screen)
+        self.last_poll = time.monotonic()
+        self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self._loop, daemon=True)
+        self.thread.start()
+
+    def set_input(self, pressed=None, held=None, mouse=None):
+        with self.lock:
+            for token in (pressed or [])[:8]:
+                if token in VALID_TOKENS:
+                    self.term.pressed.append(token)
+            if held is not None:
+                self.term.held = {t for t in held[:16] if t in VALID_TOKENS}
+            self.term.mouse.extend(_clean_mouse(mouse or []))
+            # A modal menu stops draining mouse events; keep the queue bounded
+            # so a long session in a menu cannot grow it without limit.
+            if len(self.term.mouse) > MAX_QUEUED_MOUSE:
+                del self.term.mouse[:-MAX_QUEUED_MOUSE]
+
+    def _player(self):
+        p = self.game.world.player
+        return {"x": round(p.x, 2), "z": round(p.z, 2),
+                "hp": round(p.hp, 1), "max_hp": p.max_hp,
+                "stamina": round(p.stamina, 1), "state": p.state}
+
+    def snapshot(self):
+        """Latest frame plus a small state header; marks the client alive."""
+        with self.lock:
+            self.last_poll = time.monotonic()
+            return {"status": "ready", "seq": self.seq,
+                    "w": self.width, "h": self.height,
+                    "frame": self.frame, "state": self.game.state,
+                    "weather": self.game.world.weather,
+                    "day_t": round(self.game.world.day_t, 3),
+                    "player": self._player()}
+
+    def state(self):
+        """Diagnostics for tests and the agent; does not count as a client poll."""
+        with self.lock:
+            return {"status": "ready", "seq": self.seq,
+                    "state": self.game.state,
+                    "weather": self.game.world.weather,
+                    "day_t": round(self.game.world.day_t, 3),
+                    "player": self._player()}
+
+    def step(self):
+        """One client frame: input, the shared simulation step, then render.
+
+        `Game.tick` is the same step the terminal loop runs, so a browser
+        session pauses in menus, dies and wins exactly like the terminal build.
+        """
+        with self.lock:
+            self.game._handle_keys()
+            env = self.game.tick(DT)
+            self.game._render(env)
+            self.frame = encode_frame(self.game.screen)
+            self.seq += 1
+
+    def _loop(self):
+        while self.running:
+            if time.monotonic() - self.last_poll > 20.0:
+                time.sleep(0.25)
+                continue
+            started = time.monotonic()
+            self.step()
+            rest = DT - (time.monotonic() - started)
+            if rest > 0:
+                time.sleep(rest)
+
+    def close(self):
+        self.running = False
+
+
+PLAY_HTML = """<!doctype html>
+<html lang="en">
+<head>
+<meta charset="utf-8">
+<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1">
+<title>Aldervale - Browser</title>
+<style>
+:root{--bg:#070a10;--panel:#0f1520;--line:#263043;--ink:#dce5f2;
+      --dim:#8491a6;--gold:#e8c45a;--green:#7fd18a;--red:#e07a7a}
+*{box-sizing:border-box}
+html,body{margin:0;min-height:100%;background:var(--bg);color:var(--ink)}
+body{font-family:"Cascadia Mono",Consolas,"Courier New",monospace;
+     display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;padding:14px}
+header{width:min(1400px,100%);display:flex;align-items:baseline;gap:18px;
+       flex-wrap:wrap;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);padding-bottom:8px}
+h1{font-size:16px;letter-spacing:.22em;color:var(--gold);margin:0}
+#status{color:var(--dim);font-size:12px}
+#hud{margin-left:auto;color:var(--dim);font-size:12px}
+#stage{position:relative;margin-top:14px;display:flex;align-items:center;
+       justify-content:center;min-height:240px}
+canvas{background:#05070a;border:1px solid var(--line);outline:none;
+       image-rendering:auto;display:block}
+#loading{position:absolute;inset:0;display:flex;align-items:center;
+         justify-content:center;background:rgba(5,7,10,.82);
+         color:var(--gold);font-size:14px;letter-spacing:.14em;text-align:center;
+         padding:24px}
+#loading.hide{display:none}
+footer{width:min(1400px,100%);margin-top:12px;color:var(--dim);font-size:12px;
+       line-height:1.8;display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:18px}
+a{color:var(--gold);text-decoration:none}a:hover{text-decoration:underline}
+kbd{border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:3px;padding:1px 5px;
+    color:var(--ink);font:inherit;font-size:11px}
+.note{color:#596477}
+</style>
+</head>
+<body>
+<header>
+  <h1>ALDERVALE</h1>
+  <span id="status">Connecting to the forge...</span>
+  <span id="hud"></span>
+</header>
+<div id="stage">
+  <canvas id="game" tabindex="0" aria-label="Aldervale game view"></canvas>
+  <div id="loading">FORGING THE REALM...</div>
+</div>
+<footer>
+  <span><kbd>WASD</kbd> move <kbd>arrows</kbd> move/turn
+        <kbd>SPACE</kbd> attack <kbd>SHIFT</kbd> sprint <kbd>B</kbd> guard</span>
+  <span><kbd>E</kbd> talk/take <kbd>I</kbd> bag <kbd>M</kbd> map
+        <kbd>H</kbd> help <kbd>ESC</kbd> pause</span>
+  <span>click to walk/attack . right-drag to look . wheel to zoom</span>
+  <span><a href="/studio/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Studio</a></span>
+  <span><a href="/">Launcher</a></span>
+  <span class="note">localhost thin client over the same Python engine</span>
+</footer>
+<script>
+const TOKEN = "__TOKEN__";
+const canvas = document.getElementById("game");
+const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
+const statusEl = document.getElementById("status");
+const hudEl = document.getElementById("hud");
+const loadingEl = document.getElementById("loading");
+let frame = null, lastSeq = -1, busy = false, ready = false;
+let cellW = 10, cellH = 16, dpr = 1, fitted = false;
+let held = new Set();
+let pressed = [];
+let mouse = [];
+let dragging = false;
+let framesThisSecond = 0, fps = 0, fpsAt = performance.now();
+
+function hex(c){ return "#" + c.toString(16).padStart(6, "0"); }
+
+function showLoading(text){
+  loadingEl.classList.remove("hide");
+  loadingEl.textContent = text;
+}
+
+function render(){
+  if (!frame) return;
+  const started = performance.now();
+  const w = frame.w, h = frame.h;
+  ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
+  ctx.fillStyle = "#05070a";
+  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w * cellW, h * cellH);
+  ctx.font = Math.floor(cellH * 0.92) + 'px "Cascadia Mono",Consolas,monospace';
+  ctx.textAlign = "center";
+  ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
+  for (let y = 0; y < h; y++){
+    const chars = frame.chars[y], colors = frame.colors[y];
+    let x = 0;
+    while (x < w){
+      const color = colors[x];
+      let end = x + 1;
+      while (end < w && colors[end] === color) end++;
+      ctx.fillStyle = hex(color);
+      for (let i = x; i < end; i++){
+        const ch = chars[i];
+        if (ch !== " ")
+          ctx.fillText(ch, i * cellW + cellW * 0.5,
+                       y * cellH + cellH * 0.52);
+      }
+      x = end;
+    }
+  }
+  window.__renderMs = performance.now() - started;
+}
+
+function fit(){
+  if (!frame) return;
+  const maxW = Math.max(320, innerWidth - 36);
+  const maxH = Math.max(220, innerHeight - 190);
+  let ch = Math.floor(Math.min(maxH / frame.h, maxW / (frame.w * 0.62)));
+  ch = Math.max(7, Math.min(20, ch));
+  cellH = ch;
+  cellW = Math.max(4, Math.floor(ch * 0.62));
+  dpr = Math.min(2, window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
+  canvas.width = frame.w * cellW * dpr;
+  canvas.height = frame.h * cellH * dpr;
+  canvas.style.width = (frame.w * cellW) + "px";
+  canvas.style.height = (frame.h * cellH) + "px";
+  window.__fitted = true;
+  render();
+}
+
+function updateHud(data){
+  const p = data.player || {};
+  hudEl.textContent = "HP " + Math.round(p.hp) + "/" + p.max_hp +
+    "  STA " + Math.round(p.stamina) + "  " +
+    (data.weather || "clear") + "  " + fps + " fps";
+}
+
+async function poll(){
+  if (busy) return;
+  busy = true;
+  try {
+    const r = await fetch("/api/frame", {cache: "no-store"});
+    const data = await r.json();
+    if (data.status === "ready"){
+      if (!ready){
+        ready = true;
+        showLoading("");
+        loadingEl.classList.add("hide");
+        statusEl.textContent = "Connected.";
+        canvas.focus();
+      }
+      if (data.seq !== lastSeq){
+        lastSeq = data.seq;
+        frame = {w: data.w, h: data.h, chars: data.frame.chars,
+                 colors: data.frame.colors};
+        framesThisSecond++;
+        const now = performance.now();
+        if (now - fpsAt >= 1000){
+          fps = Math.round(framesThisSecond * 1000 / (now - fpsAt));
+          framesThisSecond = 0;
+          fpsAt = now;
+        }
+        window.__frameSeq = data.seq;
+        if (!fitted){ fitted = true; fit(); } else render();
+        updateHud(data);
+      }
+    } else if (data.status === "error"){
+      showLoading("The forge failed: " + data.message);
+      statusEl.textContent = "Error.";
+    } else {
+      showLoading("FORGING THE REALM...");
+    }
+  } catch (err) {
+    showLoading("Connection lost: " + err);
+  } finally {
+    busy = false;
+  }
+}
+
+async function sendInput(){
+  if (!ready) return;
+  const body = {pressed: pressed.splice(0), held: [...held],
+                mouse: mouse.splice(0)};
+  if (!body.pressed.length && !body.mouse.length && !body.held.length &&
+      !heldChanged) return;
+  heldChanged = false;
+  try {
+    await fetch("/api/input", {
+      method: "POST",
+      headers: {"Content-Type": "application/json",
+                "X-Aldervale-Token": TOKEN},
+      body: JSON.stringify(body)
+    });
+  } catch (err) {
+    statusEl.textContent = "Input lost: " + err;
+  }
+}
+
+let heldChanged = false;
+const KEYMAP = {"ArrowUp":"up", "ArrowDown":"down", "ArrowLeft":"left",
+                "ArrowRight":"right", " ":"space", "Shift":"shift",
+                "Escape":"esc", "Enter":"enter", "Tab":"tab", "F1":"f1"};
+function tokenFor(e){
+  if (KEYMAP[e.key]) return KEYMAP[e.key];
+  if (e.key === "+" || e.key === "=") return "+";
+  if (e.key === "-" || e.key === "_") return "-";
+  if (e.key.length === 1 && /[a-z0-9]/i.test(e.key))
+    return e.key.toLowerCase();
+  return null;
+}
+function block(e){
+  return ["ArrowUp", "ArrowDown", "ArrowLeft", "ArrowRight", " ", "Tab"]
+    .includes(e.key);
+}
+window.addEventListener("keydown", e => {
+  const t = tokenFor(e);
+  if (!t) return;
+  if (block(e)) e.preventDefault();
+  if (e.repeat || held.has(t)) return;
+  held.add(t);
+  pressed.push(t);
+  heldChanged = true;
+  sendInput();
+});
+window.addEventListener("keyup", e => {
+  const t = tokenFor(e);
+  if (!t) return;
+  if (block(e)) e.preventDefault();
+  if (!held.delete(t)) return;
+  heldChanged = true;
+  sendInput();
+});
+window.addEventListener("blur", () => {
+  held.clear();
+  heldChanged = true;
+  sendInput();
+});
+
+function cellAt(e){
+  if (!frame) return [0, 0];
+  const r = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
+  return [Math.floor((e.clientX - r.left) / r.width * frame.w),
+          Math.floor((e.clientY - r.top) / r.height * frame.h)];
+}
+function flushMouse(){
+  const events = mouse.splice(0);
+  if (!events.length) return;
+  fetch("/api/input", {
+    method: "POST",
+    headers: {"Content-Type": "application/json",
+              "X-Aldervale-Token": TOKEN},
+    body: JSON.stringify({held: [...held], mouse: events})
+  }).catch(() => {});
+}
+let moveAt = 0;
+canvas.addEventListener("mousedown", e => {
+  canvas.focus();
+  const [col, row] = cellAt(e);
+  if (e.button === 0){
+    mouse.push(["ldown", col, row, 1, 0]);
+    mouse.push(["up", col, row, 0, 0]);
+    flushMouse();
+  } else if (e.button === 2){
+    dragging = true;
+    mouse.push(["rdown", col, row, 2, 0]);
+    flushMouse();
+  }
+});
+canvas.addEventListener("mousemove", e => {
+  if (!dragging) return;
+  const now = performance.now();
+  if (now - moveAt < 45) return;
+  moveAt = now;
+  const [col, row] = cellAt(e);
+  mouse.push(["move", col, row, 2, 0]);
+  flushMouse();
+});
+window.addEventListener("mouseup", e => {
+  if (e.button !== 2 || !dragging) return;
+  dragging = false;
+  const [col, row] = cellAt(e);
+  mouse.push(["up", col, row, 0, 0]);
+  flushMouse();
+});
+canvas.addEventListener("contextmenu", e => e.preventDefault());
+canvas.addEventListener("wheel", e => {
+  if (!frame) return;
+  e.preventDefault();
+  const [col, row] = cellAt(e);
+  mouse.push(["wheel", col, row, 0, e.deltaY < 0 ? 1 : -1]);
+  flushMouse();
+}, {passive: false});
+canvas.addEventListener("click", () => canvas.focus());
+window.addEventListener("resize", fit);
+
+setInterval(poll, 50);
+poll();
+</script>
+</body>
+</html>
+""".replace("__TOKEN__", "__TOKEN__")

+                else "Rain sweeps across the vale.", WEATHER_COLOR)

+class TestCapture(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_png_header_and_scanlines(self):
+        s = Screen(4, 3, color=True)
+        s.text(0, 0, "ABCD", pack_rgb(255, 0, 0))
+        s.text(0, 1, "    ", pack_rgb(8, 10, 16))
+        s.text(0, 2, "1234", pack_rgb(0, 255, 0))
+        w, h, rows = screen_pixels(s, scale=1)
+        blob = png_bytes(w, h, rows)
+        self.assertEqual(blob[:8], b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n")
+        iw, ih = struct.unpack(">II", blob[16:24])
+        self.assertEqual((iw, ih), (w, h))
+        self.assertGreater(len(rows), w * h)
+        idat_start = blob.index(b"IDAT") + 4
+        size = struct.unpack(">I", blob[idat_start - 8:idat_start - 4])[0]
+        raw = zlib.decompress(blob[idat_start:idat_start + size])
+        self.assertEqual(len(raw), h * (w * 3 + 1))
+
+    def test_glyph_pixels_use_cell_colour(self):
+        s = Screen(1, 1, color=True)
+        s.text(0, 0, "#", pack_rgb(255, 0, 0))
+        w, h, rows = screen_pixels(s, scale=1)
+        self.assertEqual(len(rows), w * h * 3)
+        self.assertTrue(any(rows[i:i + 3] == b"\xff\x00\x00"
+                            for i in range(0, len(rows), 3)))
+
+    def test_save_png_writes_file(self):
+        s = Screen(2, 1, color=True)
+        s.text(0, 0, "OK", pack_rgb(10, 200, 20))
+        d = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="aldervale_capture_")
+        path = os.path.join(d, "frame.png")
+        self.addCleanup(os.rmdir, d)
+        self.addCleanup(os.remove, path)
+        iw, ih = save_png(path, s, scale=1)
+        with open(path, "rb") as f:
+            self.assertEqual(f.read(8), b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n")
+        self.assertGreater(iw, 0)
+        self.assertGreater(ih, 0)
+
+

+class TestStudio(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_encode_screen_keeps_cells_and_colours(self):
+        s = Screen(4, 2, color=True)
+        s.text(0, 0, "AB", 0x112233)
+        frame = encode_screen(s)
+        self.assertEqual((frame["w"], frame["h"]), (4, 2))
+        self.assertTrue(frame["chars"][0].startswith("AB"))
+        self.assertEqual(frame["colors"][0][0], 0x112233)
+        self.assertEqual(len(frame["colors"]), 2)
+
+    def test_collect_models_samples_yaws_and_skips_missing(self):
+        atlas = SpriteAtlas().build(keys=["knight"])
+        data = collect_models(atlas)
+        self.assertEqual(len(data["yaw_indices"]), 8)
+        self.assertIn("knight", data["entries"])
+        self.assertNotIn("captain", data["entries"])
+        frames = data["entries"]["knight"]["idle"]
+        self.assertEqual(len(frames), 8)
+        self.assertGreater(frames[0]["w"], 0)
+
+    def test_knight_turnaround_frames_are_distinct(self):
+        atlas = SpriteAtlas().build(keys=["knight"])
+        data = collect_models(atlas)
+        frames = data["entries"]["knight"]["idle"]
+        rows = {"".join(f["chars"]) for f in frames}
+        self.assertEqual(len(rows), len(data["yaw_indices"]))
+
+    def test_write_studio_roundtrip(self):
+        import json
+        s = Screen(3, 2, color=True)
+        s.text(0, 0, "AB", 0x224466)
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
+            html, data = write_studio(d, 7, [3, 2],
+                                      [{"name": "tiny", "day_t": 0.2,
+                                        "frame": encode_screen(s)}],
+                                      {"entries": {}}, [])
+            with open(html, encoding="utf-8") as f:
+                page = f.read()
+            with open(data, encoding="utf-8") as f:
+                blob = f.read()
+        self.assertIn("Aldervale Studio", page)
+        self.assertTrue(blob.startswith("window.STUDIO = "))
+        parsed = json.loads(blob[len("window.STUDIO = "):].rstrip(";\n"))
+        self.assertEqual(parsed["scenes"][0]["name"], "tiny")
+
+    def test_find_browser_returns_none_or_a_real_path(self):
+        found = find_browser()
+        if found is not None:
+            self.assertTrue(os.path.isfile(found), found)
+
+    def test_screenshot_missing_page_fails_cleanly(self):
+        ok, detail = screenshot_studio("no/such/index.html", "shot.png")
+        self.assertFalse(ok)
+        self.assertTrue(detail)
+
+
+class TestLauncherUnit(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_fixed_launch_commands(self):
+        self.assertEqual(launcher.game_command("play"),
+                         [sys.executable, "-m", "aldervale"])
+        self.assertEqual(launcher.game_command("demo"),
+                         [sys.executable, "-m", "aldervale",
+                          "--demo", "9"])
+
+    def test_page_injects_token(self):
+        page = launcher.render_page("token-123")
+        self.assertIn("token-123", page)
+        self.assertNotIn("__TOKEN__", page)
+        self.assertIn("START GAME", page)
+        self.assertIn("/studio/", page)
+        self.assertIn("autostart", page)
+
+    def test_launcher_url_and_health_helpers(self):
+        self.assertEqual(launcher.launcher_url(8765),
+                         "http://127.0.0.1:8765/")
+        self.assertEqual(launcher.launcher_url(8765, True),
+                         "http://127.0.0.1:8765/?autostart=1")
+        self.assertFalse(launcher.check_health(0))
+
+    def test_studio_file_rejects_traversal(self):
+        self.assertIsNone(launcher._studio_file("../secrets.txt"))
+        self.assertIsNone(launcher._studio_file("/etc/passwd"))
+        self.assertIsNone(launcher._studio_file(".hidden"))
+
+
+class TestWebGameUnit(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_encode_frame_returns_rows(self):
+        s = Screen(4, 2, color=True)
+        s.text(0, 0, "AB", 0x123456)
+        frame = encode_frame(s)
+        self.assertEqual(frame["chars"][0][:2], "AB")
+        self.assertEqual(frame["colors"][0][0], 0x123456)
+        self.assertEqual(len(frame["colors"]), 2)
+
+    def test_web_term_queues_input_and_ignores_output(self):
+        term = WebTerm(80, 40)
+        term.pressed.extend(["w", "space"])
+        term.held = {"w", "shift"}
+        term.mouse = [("ldown", 4, 5, 1, 0)]
+        pressed, held = term.poll()
+        self.assertEqual(pressed, ["w", "space"])
+        self.assertEqual(held, {"w", "shift"})
+        self.assertEqual(term.poll(), ([], {"w", "shift"}))
+        self.assertEqual(term.take_mouse(), [("ldown", 4, 5, 1, 0)])
+        self.assertEqual(term.take_mouse(), [])
+        self.assertEqual(term.size(), (80, 40))
+        self.assertIsNone(term.flush())
+
+    def test_queued_mouse_events_stay_bounded(self):
+        import threading
+
+        from aldervale.webgame import MAX_QUEUED_MOUSE, WebGame
+
+        session = object.__new__(WebGame)
+        session.lock = threading.RLock()
+        session.term = WebTerm(80, 40)
+        burst = [["move", 10, 10, 2, 0] for _ in range(8)]
+        for _ in range(40):
+            session.set_input(pressed=[], held=[], mouse=burst)
+        self.assertEqual(len(session.term.mouse), MAX_QUEUED_MOUSE)
+        self.assertEqual(session.term.mouse[-1], ("move", 10, 10, 2, 0),
+                         "the newest events survive the cap")
+

+class FakeWebSession:
+    def __init__(self):
+        self.seq = 3
+        self.inputs = []
+
+    def snapshot(self):
+        return {"status": "ready", "seq": self.seq, "w": 2, "h": 1,
+                "frame": {"chars": ["AB"], "colors": [[1, 2]]},
+                "state": "play", "weather": "clear", "day_t": 0.2,
+                "player": {"x": 1.0, "z": 2.0, "hp": 46, "max_hp": 46,
+                           "stamina": 100, "state": "idle"}}
+
+    def state(self):
+        data = self.snapshot()
+        data.pop("frame", None)
+        return data
+
+    def set_input(self, pressed, held, mouse):
+        self.inputs.append((pressed, held, mouse))
+
+
+class TestLauncherHTTP(unittest.TestCase):
+    def setUp(self):
+        import threading
+        from http.server import ThreadingHTTPServer
+        from aldervale import launcher
+        self.launcher = launcher
+        self.calls = []
+        self.server = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), launcher.Handler)
+        self.server.daemon_threads = True
+        self.server.spawn = lambda mode: (self.calls.append(mode) or
+                                          (True, "spawned " + mode))
+        self.server.last_spawn = 0.0
+        self.server.last_mode = None
+        self.server.launch_lock = threading.Lock()
+        self.server.web_lock = threading.Lock()
+        self.server.web_error = None
+        self.server.web_building = False
+        self.server.web_session = FakeWebSession()
+        self._fake = self.server
+        self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self.server.serve_forever,
+                                       daemon=True)
+        self.thread.start()
+        self.port = self.server.server_address[1]
+        self.base = "http://127.0.0.1:%d" % self.port
+
+        def stop():
+            self.server.shutdown()
+            self.thread.join(5)
+            self.server.server_close()
+        self.addCleanup(stop)
+
+    def _post(self, payload, token=None):
+        import urllib.request
+        headers = {"Content-Type": "application/json"}
+        if token:
+            headers["X-Aldervale-Token"] = token
+        req = urllib.request.Request(self.base + "/start",
+                                     data=json.dumps(payload).encode(),
+                                     headers=headers, method="POST")
+        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=5) as r:
+            return r.status, json.loads(r.read())
+
+    def _post_api(self, payload, token=None):
+        import urllib.request
+        headers = {"Content-Type": "application/json"}
+        if token:
+            headers["X-Aldervale-Token"] = token
+        req = urllib.request.Request(self.base + "/api/input",
+                                     data=json.dumps(payload).encode(),
+                                     headers=headers, method="POST")
+        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=5) as r:
+            return r.status, json.loads(r.read())
+
+    def test_play_page_frame_and_state(self):
+        import urllib.request
+        with urllib.request.urlopen(self.base + "/play", timeout=5) as r:
+            page = r.read().decode()
+        self.assertIn("<canvas", page)
+        self.assertIn(self.launcher.TOKEN, page)
+        self.assertIn("/api/frame", page)
+        with urllib.request.urlopen(self.base + "/api/frame", timeout=5) as r:
+            data = json.loads(r.read())
+        self.assertEqual(data["status"], "ready")
+        self.assertEqual(data["seq"], 3)
+        with urllib.request.urlopen(self.base + "/api/state", timeout=5) as r:
+            state = json.loads(r.read())
+        self.assertEqual(state["player"]["x"], 1.0)
+        self.assertNotIn("frame", state)
+
+    def test_input_api_forwards_and_requires_token(self):
+        import urllib.error
+        with self.assertRaises(urllib.error.HTTPError) as ctx:
+            self._post_api({"held": ["w"]})
+        self.assertEqual(ctx.exception.code, 403)
+        ctx.exception.close()
+        self._post_api({"pressed": ["space"], "held": ["w", "shift"],
+                        "mouse": [["ldown", 4, 5, 1, 0]]},
+                       self.launcher.TOKEN)
+        pressed, held, mouse = self._fake.web_session.inputs[-1]
+        self.assertEqual(pressed, ["space"])
+        self.assertEqual(held, ["w", "shift"])
+        self.assertEqual(mouse, [["ldown", 4, 5, 1, 0]])
+
+    def test_frame_reports_loading_before_session(self):
+        import urllib.request
+        self._fake.web_session = None
+        self._fake.web_building = True
+        with urllib.request.urlopen(self.base + "/api/frame", timeout=5) as r:
+            data = json.loads(r.read())
+        self.assertEqual(data["status"], "loading")
+
+    def test_page_and_token_protected_start(self):
+        import urllib.error
+        import urllib.request
+        with urllib.request.urlopen(self.base + "/", timeout=5) as r:
+            page = r.read().decode()
+        self.assertEqual(r.status, 200)
+        self.assertIn("START GAME", page)
+        self.assertIn(self.launcher.TOKEN, page)
+        with self.assertRaises(urllib.error.HTTPError) as ctx:
+            self._post({"mode": "play"})
+        self.assertEqual(ctx.exception.code, 403)
+        ctx.exception.close()
+        status, data = self._post({"mode": "demo"}, self.launcher.TOKEN)
+        self.assertEqual(status, 200)
+        self.assertTrue(data["ok"])
+        self.assertEqual(self.calls, ["demo"])
+        status, data = self._post({"mode": "play"}, self.launcher.TOKEN)
+        self.assertTrue(data["ok"])
+        self.assertEqual(self.calls, ["demo", "play"])
+        self.assertTrue(self.launcher.check_health(self.port))
+
+    def test_duplicate_start_is_coalesced(self):
+        status, data = self._post({"mode": "play"}, self.launcher.TOKEN)
+        self.assertTrue(data["ok"])
+        status, data = self._post({"mode": "play"}, self.launcher.TOKEN)
+        self.assertTrue(data["ok"])
+        self.assertIn("already", data["message"].lower())
+        self.assertEqual(self.calls, ["play"])
+
+    def test_health_and_unknown_path(self):
+        import urllib.error
+        import urllib.request
+        with urllib.request.urlopen(self.base + "/health", timeout=5) as r:
+            self.assertEqual(r.read(), b"ok")
+        with self.assertRaises(urllib.error.HTTPError) as ctx:
+            urllib.request.urlopen(self.base + "/nope", timeout=5)
+        self.assertEqual(ctx.exception.code, 404)
+        ctx.exception.close()
+
+
+class TestCamera(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_default_camera_is_close_enough_for_model_detail(self):
+        g = Game(seed=3, terrain_size=SMALL, width=132, height=60)
+        self.assertLessEqual(g.cam_dist, 5.0)
+        self.assertGreaterEqual(g.cam_dist, 4.0)
+        self.assertGreaterEqual(g.cam_ease_d, 3.0)
+
+    def test_camera_pulls_in_when_a_hill_blocks(self):
+        from aldervale.render import clear_camera_distance
+
+        class Ridge:
+            def height(self, x, z):
+                return 8.0 if -5.0 < z < -2.5 else 0.0
+
+        clear = clear_camera_distance(Ridge(), 0.0, 0.0, 1.55, 0.0, 1.0,
+                                      3.0, 4.6)
+        self.assertLess(clear, 4.6)
+        self.assertGreaterEqual(clear, 2.2)
+
+    def test_camera_keeps_full_distance_on_flat_ground(self):
+        from aldervale.render import clear_camera_distance
+
+        class Flat:
+            def height(self, x, z):
+                return 0.0
+
+        self.assertEqual(
+            clear_camera_distance(Flat(), 0.0, 0.0, 1.55, 0.0, 1.0,
+                                  3.0, 4.6), 4.6)
+

+class TestInspection(unittest.TestCase):
+    """The headless lens: PNG scenes, text mirrors and the control probe."""
+
+    KEYS = ["knight", "wolf", "rock_s", "gold", "house1"]
+
+    def test_inspection_writes_png_text_and_report(self):
+        out = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="aldervale_inspect_")
+        self.addCleanup(_rmtree, out)
+        report = run_inspection(out, seed=3, width=64, height=30,
+                                terrain_size=SMALL, scale=1,
+                                scenes=["village", "rain"], probe=False,
+                                atlas_keys=self.KEYS)
+        self.assertEqual(len(report["scenes"]), 2)
+        for name in ("02_village.png", "02_village.txt", "18_rain.png",
+                     "18_rain.txt", "report.json", "index.html",
+                     "studio-data.js"):
+            self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(os.path.join(out, name)), name)
+        with open(os.path.join(out, "02_village.png"), "rb") as f:
+            self.assertEqual(f.read(8), b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n")
+        with open(os.path.join(out, "report.json"), encoding="utf-8") as f:
+            self.assertIn('"viewport"', f.read())
+
+    def test_probe_controls_all_pass(self):
+        game = build_game(seed=3, width=80, height=40,
+                          terrain_size=SMALL, atlas_keys=self.KEYS)
+        failed = [c["name"] for c in probe_controls(game) if not c["ok"]]
+        self.assertEqual(failed, [])
+
