+class TestResponsiveMenus(unittest.TestCase):
+    """Every menu panel must stay inside its own box at any viewport size."""
+
+    WIDTHS = (40, 48, 64, 80, 132)
+
+    def _screen(self, width, height=30):
+        return Screen(width, height, color=True)
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _find_box(scr):
+        for y in range(scr.h):
+            row = "".join(scr.chars[y])
+            if "+" not in row or "-" not in row:
+                continue
+            left = row.index("+")
+            right = row.rindex("+")
+            if right - left < 6:
+                continue
+            bottom = y
+            for yy in range(y + 1, scr.h):
+                if scr.chars[yy][left] == "+" and scr.chars[yy][right] == "+":
+                    bottom = yy
+            return left, y, right, bottom
+        return None
+
+    def _assert_panel_contained(self, scr, label):
+        box = self._find_box(scr)
+        self.assertIsNotNone(box, "%s: no panel box drawn" % label)
+        left, top, right, bottom = box
+        self.assertGreaterEqual(left, 0, label)
+        self.assertLess(right, scr.w, label)
+        self.assertGreaterEqual(top, 0, label)
+        self.assertLess(bottom, scr.h, label)
+        for y in range(top + 1, bottom):
+            self.assertEqual(scr.chars[y][left], "|",
+                             "%s: left border broken at row %d" % (label, y))
+            self.assertEqual(scr.chars[y][right], "|",
+                             "%s: right border broken at row %d" % (label, y))
+        for y in list(range(0, top)) + list(range(bottom + 1, scr.h)):
+            self.assertEqual("".join(scr.chars[y]).strip(), "",
+                             "%s: content outside the panel at row %d"
+                             % (label, y))
+        for y in range(top, bottom + 1):
+            for x in list(range(0, left)) + list(range(right + 1, scr.w)):
+                self.assertEqual(scr.chars[y][x], " ",
+                                 "%s: glyph outside the panel at %d,%d"
+                                 % (label, x, y))
+
+    def test_help_fits_every_width(self):
+        from aldervale import hud
+        for width in self.WIDTHS:
+            scr = self._screen(width)
+            hud.draw_help(scr)
+            self._assert_panel_contained(scr, "help@%d" % width)
+            text = "\n".join("".join(row) for row in scr.chars)
+            self.assertIn("pause", text, "help@%d lost a control line"
+                          % width)
+
+    def test_inventory_fits_every_width(self):
+        from aldervale import hud
+        w = World(3, None, terrain_size=SMALL)
+        p = w.player
+        for item_id in ("iron_sword", "leather_armor", "potion", "pelt"):
+            p.inventory.add(item_id, 1)
+        for width in self.WIDTHS:
+            scr = self._screen(width)
+            hud.draw_inventory(scr, w, "U use a potion   E equip the best")
+            self._assert_panel_contained(scr, "inventory@%d" % width)
+            text = "\n".join("".join(row) for row in scr.chars)
+            self.assertIn("SATCHEL", text, "inventory@%d lost its title"
+                          % width)
+            self.assertIn("[I] or [ESC] close", text,
+                          "inventory@%d lost its close hint" % width)
+
+    def test_shop_fits_every_width(self):
+        from aldervale import hud
+        w = World(3, None, terrain_size=SMALL)
+        for width in self.WIDTHS:
+            scr = self._screen(width)
+            hud.draw_shop(scr, w, "buy", 6, "Bram eyes your purse.")
+            self._assert_panel_contained(scr, "shop@%d" % width)
+            text = "\n".join("".join(row) for row in scr.chars)
+            self.assertIn("Healing Draught", text,
+                          "shop@%d cut the last ware" % width)
+
+    def test_shop_sell_fits_every_width(self):
+        from aldervale import hud
+        for width in self.WIDTHS:
+            w = World(3, None, terrain_size=SMALL)
+            for item_id in ("pelt", "bone", "potion"):
+                w.player.inventory.add(item_id, 2)
+            scr = self._screen(width)
+            hud.draw_shop(scr, w, "sell", 1, "Sold a pelt.")
+            self._assert_panel_contained(scr, "sell@%d" % width)
+            text = "\n".join("".join(row) for row in scr.chars)
+            self.assertIn("Your goods", text, "sell@%d lost its header"
+                          % width)
+
+    def test_overlay_boxes_fit_every_width(self):
+        from aldervale import hud
+        w = World(3, None, terrain_size=SMALL)
+        for width in self.WIDTHS:
+            for name, draw in (
+                    ("pause", lambda s: hud.draw_pause(s, 1)),
+                    ("death", lambda s: hud.draw_death(s, w)),
+                    ("victory", lambda s: hud.draw_victory(s, w, 2.0))):
+                scr = self._screen(width)
+                draw(scr)
+                self._assert_panel_contained(scr, "%s@%d" % (name, width))
+
+    def test_title_fits_every_width(self):
+        from aldervale import hud
+        from aldervale.render import Camera, Env
+        for width in self.WIDTHS:
+            scr = self._screen(width)
+            w = World(3, None, terrain_size=SMALL)
+            cam = Camera(width, scr.h, 0.0, 0.0, 5.0, 0.0)
+            hud.draw_title(scr, w, cam, Env(0.25, 0.0), 0, True)
+            rows = ["".join(row) for row in scr.chars]
+            for i, row in enumerate(rows):
+                if row.strip():
+                    self.assertLessEqual(len(row.rstrip()), width,
+                                         "title@%d overflows on row %d"
+                                         % (width, i))
+            text = "\n".join(rows)
+            self.assertIn("WASD walk", text,
+                          "title@%d lost its control footer" % width)
+            self.assertIn("ALDERVALE v1.0", text,
+                          "title@%d lost its version line" % width)
+

+    def test_readme_lists_every_module_and_license_files(self):

+        self.assertIn("## License", readme, "the README needs a License section")
+        self.assertNotIn("Aegean-E/AIKit", readme,
+                         "the badge must point at the current AIKit repository")

+class TestMechanics(unittest.TestCase):
+    """Combat simulation mechanics: guard, facing, backstab, potions, respawn."""
+
+    def _world(self):
+        return World(3, None, terrain_size=SMALL)
+
+    def test_returning_enemy_does_not_instant_heal(self):
+        w = self._world()
+        skel = next(e for e in w.entities
+                    if getattr(e, "kind", "") == "skeleton")
+        skel.hp = 5
+        skel.state = "return"
+        env = Env(0.25, 0.0)
+        for _ in range(30):
+            w.update(1.0 / 30.0, env)
+        self.assertLessEqual(skel.hp, 6)
+
+    def test_guard_reduces_damage_and_costs_stamina(self):
+        from aldervale.entities import Actor
+        w = self._world()
+        p = w.player
+        p.hp = 100
+        p.max_hp = 100
+        p.guarding = True
+        p.stamina = 100.0
+        attacker = Actor("att", "wolf", p.x + 1.0, p.z, 1.0, kind="wolf",
+                         hp=5, atk=5, dfn=0, speed=1.0, xp_reward=0,
+                         aggro_r=0.0, attack_range=1.0, attack_dmg=20,
+                         name="Test")
+        w.hit_player(20, attacker)
+        guarded = 100 - p.hp
+        w2 = self._world()
+        p2 = w2.player
+        p2.hp = 100
+        p2.max_hp = 100
+        p2.guarding = False
+        w2.hit_player(20, attacker)
+        open_loss = 100 - p2.hp
+        self.assertLess(guarded, open_loss * 0.6)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(p.stamina, 90.0)
+
+    def test_guard_needs_stamina(self):
+        from aldervale.entities import Actor
+        w = self._world()
+        p = w.player
+        p.hp = 100
+        p.max_hp = 100
+        p.guarding = True
+        p.stamina = 5.0
+        attacker = Actor("att", "wolf", p.x + 1.0, p.z, 1.0, kind="wolf",
+                         hp=5, atk=5, dfn=0, speed=1.0, xp_reward=0,
+                         aggro_r=0.0, attack_range=1.0, attack_dmg=20,
+                         name="Test")
+        w.hit_player(20, attacker)
+        self.assertGreater(100 - p.hp, 8)
+
+    def test_enemy_strike_requires_facing(self):
+        w = self._world()
+        p = w.player
+        p.hp = 100
+        p.max_hp = 100
+        skel = next(e for e in w.entities
+                    if getattr(e, "kind", "") == "skeleton")
+        skel.hp = 999999
+        skel.x, skel.z = p.x, p.z + 1.2
+        skel.state = "windup"
+        skel.windup_t = skel.windup_s - 0.01
+        skel.yaw = math.pi
+        env = Env(0.25, 0.0)
+        w.update(1.0 / 30.0, env)
+        self.assertLess(p.hp, 100)
+        p.hp = 100
+        skel.hp = 999999
+        skel.x, skel.z = p.x, p.z + 1.2
+        skel.state = "windup"
+        skel.windup_t = skel.windup_s - 0.01
+        skel.yaw = 0.0
+        w.update(1.0 / 30.0, env)
+        self.assertEqual(p.hp, 100)
+
+    def test_backstab_deals_bonus(self):
+        w = self._world()
+        p = w.player
+        foe = next(e for e in w.entities
+                   if getattr(e, "kind", "") == "skeleton")
+        foe.x, foe.z = p.x, p.z + 1.2
+        foe.yaw = 0.0
+        foe.hp = 500
+        p.yaw = 0.0
+        w.resolve_player_swing()
+        back = 500 - foe.hp
+        w2 = self._world()
+        p2 = w2.player
+        foe2 = next(e for e in w2.entities
+                    if getattr(e, "kind", "") == "skeleton")
+        foe2.x, foe2.z = p2.x, p2.z + 1.2
+        foe2.yaw = math.pi
+        foe2.hp = 500
+        p2.yaw = 0.0
+        w2.resolve_player_swing()
+        front = 500 - foe2.hp
+        self.assertGreater(back, front)
+
+    def test_potion_heals_over_time(self):
+        g = Game(seed=3, terrain_size=SMALL, width=80, height=40)
+        g.term = None
+        g.atlas = _tiny_atlas()
+        g.world = World(3, g.atlas, terrain_size=SMALL)
+        g._open_screen()
+        g._ensure_camera()
+        g.state = "play"
+        g.dt = 1.0 / 30.0
+        p = g.world.player
+        p.inventory.add("potion", 1)
+        p.hp = 5
+        g._use_potion()
+        self.assertEqual(p.inventory.count("potion"), 1)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(p.hp, 5.0)
+        env = Env(0.25, 0.0)
+        for _ in range(30):
+            g.world.update(1.0 / 30.0, env)
+        self.assertGreater(p.hp, 10)
+        self.assertLess(p.hp, p.max_hp + 0.01)
+        for _ in range(90):
+            g.world.update(1.0 / 30.0, env)
+        self.assertAlmostEqual(p.hp, p.max_hp, places=2)
+
+    def test_sell_menu_ignores_currency(self):
+        w = self._world()
+        ids = [iid for iid, _n in w.sellables()]
+        self.assertNotIn("gold", ids)
+        self.assertIn("potion", ids)
+        self.assertFalse(w.sell("gold"))
+        self.assertTrue(w.sell("potion"))
+        self.assertFalse(w.sell("amulet"))
+        self.assertFalse(w.sell("not_an_item"))
+
+    def test_killed_enemy_respawns_far_away(self):
+        w = self._world()
+        skel = next(e for e in w.entities
+                    if getattr(e, "kind", "") == "skeleton")
+        living_before = len([e for e in w.entities
+                             if getattr(e, "kind", "") == "skeleton"
+                             and not getattr(e, "dead", False)])
+        w.kill_actor(skel)
+        self.assertTrue(any(r["kind"] == "skeleton" for r in w.respawn))
+        w.respawn[-1]["t"] = 0.1
+        p = w.player
+        p.x, p.z = 4.0, 4.0
+        env = Env(0.25, 0.0)
+        w.update(0.2, env)
+        living_after = len([e for e in w.entities
+                            if getattr(e, "kind", "") == "skeleton"
+                            and not getattr(e, "dead", False)])
+        self.assertEqual(living_after, living_before)
+
+
+class TestSharedSimulationStep(unittest.TestCase):
+    """The browser session must step the world exactly like the terminal loop."""
+
+    def _game(self):
+        game = Game(seed=3, terrain_size=SMALL, width=80, height=40)
+        game.world = World(3, None, terrain_size=SMALL)
+        game.screen = Screen(80, 40, color=True)
+        game.term = None
+        game.state = "play"
+        return game
+
+    def test_a_menu_freezes_the_world(self):
+        game = self._game()
+        game.state = "inventory"
+        before = game.world.time
+        game.tick(0.1)
+        self.assertEqual(game.world.time, before,
+                         "menus must pause the simulation, as in the terminal loop")
+        game.state = "play"
+        game.tick(0.1)
+        self.assertGreater(game.world.time, before)
+
+    def test_a_fallen_hero_enters_the_death_state(self):
+        game = self._game()
+        game.world.player.state = "dead"
+        game.tick(0.05)
+        self.assertEqual(game.state, "dead",
+                         "the browser needs the death screen to offer respawn")
+
+    def test_victory_arms_once_and_then_counts_up(self):
+        game = self._game()
+        game.world.quest = "done"
+        game.tick(0.05)
+        self.assertTrue(game.world.victory_shown)
+        first = game.victory_t
+        self.assertGreater(first, 0.0)
+        game.world.quest = "done"
+        game.tick(0.05)
+        self.assertGreater(game.victory_t, first,
+                           "the overlay clock keeps moving after it is armed")
+
+    def test_the_web_session_steps_through_the_shared_tick(self):
+        import threading
+        from types import SimpleNamespace
+
+        calls = {}
+
+        class FakeScreen:
+            chars = [list("AB"), list("  ")]
+            fg = [[0x111111, 0x222222], [0, 0]]
+
+        def tick(dt):
+            calls["tick"] = dt
+            return "env"
+
+        game = SimpleNamespace(
+            _handle_keys=lambda: calls.__setitem__("keys", True),
+            tick=tick,
+            _render=lambda env: calls.__setitem__("render", env),
+            screen=FakeScreen(),
+        )
+        session = object.__new__(WebGame)
+        session.game = game
+        session.lock = threading.RLock()
+        session.seq = 0
+        session.frame = None
+        session.step()
+        self.assertTrue(calls.get("keys"), "input is handled first")
+        self.assertEqual(calls.get("tick"), 1.0 / 20.0)
+        self.assertEqual(calls.get("render"), "env")
+        self.assertEqual(session.seq, 1)
+        self.assertEqual(session.frame["chars"], ["AB", "  "])
+
 
 if __name__ == "__main__":
     unittest.main()

hunk 9039f58d9d93:.aikit/decisions.md#1 (new lines 192..200)
@@ -192,3 +192,9 @@ Context, Decision and Consequences lines.
 - **Decision**: Extract `Game.tick(dt)` as the single simulation step: build the frame environment, advance the world only in `play`/`title`/`dead`, sync the death state, arm the victory overlay once and count its clock, and tick the fps counter. `Game.run` and `WebGame` (through a new `WebGame.step`) both call it. Bound the browser mouse queue at 64 events, keeping the newest.
 - **Consequences**: Both builds now pause, die and win identically, and the terminal loop keeps its behaviour. `Game.tick` is covered directly (menu freeze, death transition, victory clock) and `WebGame.step` is covered by a fake game, so the delegation cannot silently drift again. A live Edge run confirmed the world clock stops in the inventory and resumes on close, a forced death reaches the `dead` state and respawns on Enter, and the victory overlay arms with a moving clock. 169 tests and 4/4 gates.
 - Cross-principle check: no principle section changed. Serves PRINCIPLE-001 (one code path), PRINCIPLE-014 (failing-first coverage of the divergence) and PRINCIPLE-015 (live dogfooding of the browser build).
+
+## Licensing migration (agpl-final)
+
+- New versions use the Aegean Game License 1.1 (AGL-1.1); versions up to and including `agpl-final` remain under GNU AGPL-3.0.
+- Rights holder: AegeanWorks; commercial use under ACL-1.0.
+- Counsel review of the 1.1 texts remains pending.

 ---
 
-## 1. Portfolio Licensing Architecture
+## Tiers
 
-Every project across the Aegean-E portfolio operates under a unified, compatible two-tier model:
+| Tier | Who it is for | Terms | Cost |
+|---|---|---|---|
+| **Personal** | One individual, non-commercial use: learning, coursework, hobby projects, personal research | Aegean Game License 1.1 (AGL-1.1) | **Free** |
+| **Commercial** | Companies and every other organization, freelancers, employees at work, hosting, paid client work | ACL-1.0, evidenced by a License Grant Certificate | **Paid, quoted**; one 28-day non-production evaluation per company group |
+| **Restricted** | Projects withdrawn from distribution | All Rights Reserved notice from a recorded cutoff | Not offered publicly |
 
-```mermaid
-flowchart TD
-    Project["Any Aegean-E Project (Code + Assets)"]
-    
-    Project --> Code["Software Code & Logic"]
-    Project --> Assets["Creative & Intellectual Assets<br/>(Art, Audio, Data, Templates, Prompts)"]
-    
-    Code --> AGPL["Public Tier: GNU AGPL-3.0<br/>• Full source code open<br/>• Network copyleft enforced<br/>• Free for FOSS & personal use"]
-    Assets --> NC["Public Tier: Non-Commercial / All Rights Reserved<br/>• Free for non-commercial play/study<br/>• Commercial copying forbidden"]
-    
-    Code --> AECL["Commercial Tier: Aegean-E Commercial License (AECL)<br/>• Waives AGPL-3.0 copyleft<br/>• Closed-source & proprietary rights<br/>• Commercial distribution & SaaS authorized"]
-    Assets --> AECL
-```
-
-1. **Open-Source Community Tier (GNU AGPL-3.0):**
-   * The source code of all portfolio projects is provided under the **[GNU AGPL-3.0](LICENSE)**. Anyone is free to inspect, run, modify, and build upon the software, provided all downstream distributions and network-hosted services share their complete source code under AGPL-3.0.
-2. **Creative & Domain Asset Protection:**
-   * Non-code assets—including 2D/3D game art, audio, textures, medical/clinical templates, specialized prompt engineering capsules, and proprietary datasets—are strictly protected against unauthorized commercial resale, repackaging, or extraction.
-3. **Aegean-E Commercial License (AECL):**
-   * For organizations, businesses, game publishers, and SaaS providers requiring closed-source deployment, proprietary embedding, or commercial asset utilization, an AECL provides a complete legal waiver.
-
----
-
-## 2. Universal Commercial Tiers Across All Projects
-
-| Licensing Tier | Community (Free) | Commercial Pro | Enterprise Site | OEM & Game Publishing |
-|---|---|---|---|---|
-| **Coverage** | Any Aegean-E Project | Single Project / Startup | Full Portfolio Access | Commercial Distribution |
-| **Eligibility** | FOSS / Personal / Research | Startups & Growing Teams | Enterprises & Corporations | Publishers & Hardware Vendors |
-| **Pricing & Terms** | **Free (AGPL-3.0)** | **Negotiated / Tailored Quote** | **Negotiated / Enterprise Agreement** | **Custom Royalty / Revenue Share** |
-| **AGPL-3.0 Source Disclosure** | ❌ Mandatory (Section 13) | ✅ Waived for this project | ✅ Waived across all projects | ✅ Waived completely |
-| **Closed-Source SaaS / Hosting** | ❌ Prohibited without source | ✅ Allowed | ✅ Allowed | ✅ Allowed |
-| **Commercial Asset Usage** | ❌ Non-commercial only | ✅ Commercial use granted | ✅ Full commercial rights | ✅ Full commercial rights |
-| **Redistribution Rights** | Copyleft only | Private client deliverables | Enterprise subsidiaries | White-label / Retail release |
-| **Warranty & Indemnification** | ❌ As-is | Standard Commercial | Full Legal Indemnification | Full Legal Indemnification |
-
----
-
-## 3. Project-Specific Applications & Compatibility
-
-Because the portfolio spans multiple domains, the AECL applies consistently according to the project's nature:
-
-### A. Developer Tooling & Infrastructure
-* **Under AGPL-3.0:** Developers can automate workflows and build tooling freely, provided derived tools and network services disclose source.
-* **Under AECL:** Organizations can integrate tools into internal CI/CD pipelines, commercial platforms, and client deliverables without disclosing proprietary source code or operational logic.
-
-### B. AI & Computational Engines
-* **Under AGPL-3.0:** Researchers and students can study, run, and extend computational and neural architectures.
-* **Under AECL:** Commercial entities can deploy engines inside proprietary devices, closed-source software, or paid APIs without copyleft disclosure.
-
-### C. Games & Interactive Media
-* **Under AGPL-3.0:** Game engine code and gameplay logic are open for study and non-commercial modding.
-* **Asset Protection:** All visual assets, 3D models, audio, music, lore, and creative content are protected proprietary assets. Commercial distribution, retail packaging, or store publishing requires an AECL Commercial Agreement.
-
-### D. Specialized Domain Data & Clinical Templates
-* **Under AGPL-3.0:** Open for peer review, educational study, and non-profit research use.
-* **Under AECL:** Commercial vendors, private practices, and commercial platforms can integrate templates into proprietary systems without disclosing confidential patient or organizational infrastructure.
-
----
-
-## 4. Rights Granted Under the Aegean-E Commercial License
-
-An active AECL grants the purchasing licensee:
-
-1. **Full AGPL-3.0 Waiver:** Absolute freedom from any obligation to release, publish, or license your proprietary application or backend source code.
-2. **Asset Commercialization Grant:** Legal permission to use, display, bundle, and distribute project assets (graphics, prompts, rules, clinical workflows) in commercial products.
-3. **Confidentiality Assurance:** Freedom to deploy within closed internal networks, proprietary clouds, or client engagements under NDA.
-4. **Perpetual Version Deployment:** Any version of an Aegean-E project released during your active subscription remains licensed for production perpetually.
-
----
-
-## 5. Restrictions (Even with AECL)
-
-* **No Brand Hijacking:** You may not market your products under "Aegean-E" or project-specific brand names in a way that suggests endorsement or affiliation.
-* **No Raw Standalone Reselling:** You cannot take the unmodified raw repository and resell it as a standalone license without adding substantial application value.
-
----
-
-## 6. Enterprise Procurement & Contact
-
-* **Direct Inquiry:** `aegean-licensing@proton.me`
-* **Custom Quotes & Commercial Discussion:** Commercial terms, scope of use, and fees are tailored to your deployment scale, team size, and commercial model. Inquire directly with project details.
-* **Procurement Support:** Master Service Agreements (MSA), W-9 / Tax Residence Certificates, ACH, Wire, SEPA, and Net-30 PO processing.

+# Aegean Game License 1.1 (AGL-1.1)
+
+**Version 1.1 — October 2026**
+**Licensor**: AegeanWorks (`aegean-licensing@proton.me`), Türkiye
+**SPDX identifier**: `LicenseRef-AGL-1.1`
+**Base license**: Aegean License 1.1 (AL-1.1), `LicenseRef-AL-1.1`
+
+Required Notice: Copyright (c) AegeanWorks. Licensed under the Aegean Game License 1.1 (AGL-1.1). Commercial use requires the Aegean Commercial License 1.0 (ACL-1.0): aegean-licensing@proton.me
+
+> **Counsel note.** AGL-1.1 combines AL-1.1 with the Game Terms below. It should be reviewed by
+> qualified counsel before it is relied on in a dispute. If a term here conflicts with AL-1.1,
+> this document controls for games; otherwise AL-1.1 applies unchanged.
+
+---
+
+## 1. Scope and Definitions
+
+1.1 **Covered Game** means the source code, object code, documentation and Game Assets that a
+project identifies as offered under AGL-1.1. A project does this by carrying this text as its
+`LICENSE` and naming AGL-1.1 in its README release notes.
+
+1.2 **Game Assets** (art, models, animation, audio, music, voice, fonts, level data and narrative)
+are Covered Material only when this `LICENSE` names them, or when the project names an **Asset
+Manifest** (for example `ASSETS.md`) that lists them and their terms. Assets not listed remain
+proprietary: You may use them only as part of playing the Covered Game, and you may not extract,
+redistribute or include them in a mod without a grant.
+
+1.3 **Third-Party Components** (engines, libraries, fonts, audio, art, tools and platform SDKs)
+keep their own licenses. AGL-1.1 grants only what Licensor owns or may sublicense; it does not
+replace a third party's terms.
+
+1.4 **Non-Commercial** means not primarily intended for commercial advantage or monetary
+compensation, and not Commercial or Organizational Use under AL-1.1 Section 4.1. Personal
+recording and streaming allowed by Section 2.2 stay non-commercial even when the channel is
+monetized.
+
+1.5 Every other capitalized term has the meaning AL-1.1 gives it. AL-1.1 Sections 6 (Patent
+Defense), 7 (Ownership and Contributions) and 8 to 15 apply to AGL-1.1 as if written here, except
+where Sections 2 to 4 below add game terms; a conflict is resolved in favour of this document.
+AL-1.1 Sections 3 to 5 apply only as modified by Sections 2 to 4 below.
+
+## 2. Personal Play and Creation
+
+Subject to AL-1.1 Section 3 and this document, Licensor grants You a personal, non-commercial
+right to:
+
+1. download, install, run, play and replay the Covered Game, online or offline, on devices You own
+   or control, and keep reasonable backup copies;
+2. record, stream, screenshot and share gameplay, reviews and commentary, including on monetized
+   channels, provided You do not distribute the game itself and do not imply Licensor endorses
+   You;
+3. run a private multiplayer server for personal play with people You invite; a public, paid or
+   commercial server needs a commercial license;
+4. mod, patch and adapt the Covered Game for Your own private use, and create fan art, fan fiction
+   and original music inspired by the game that does not copy Game Assets; and
+5. publish and share a mod, patch or code change free of charge when it requires the player to own
+   a licensed copy and contains no extracted Game Assets. A paid mod, a standalone asset pack, or a
+   mod that includes Game Assets needs a commercial license and an asset grant.
+
+You retain ownership of Your mods and other original work. This License does not grant Licensor
+any rights in that original content; including it in the Covered Game needs a separate agreement.
+Player save data, screenshots, recordings and streams You make are Your own work, and this License
+claims no ownership of them.
+
+### 2.6 Patent License
+
+Licensor grants You a non-exclusive, worldwide, royalty-free patent license under patent claims
+Licensor owns or can license that are necessarily infringed by the Covered Game as Licensor
+provides it, solely to exercise the rights Section 2 grants. This patent license does not cover
+patent claims in Your mods or third-party components. It terminates if You or Your Company make a
+written claim that the Covered Game infringes a patent, as AL-1.1 Section 6 provides.
+
+### 2.7 Lawful Use
+
+You must comply with applicable law and must not use the Covered Game for unlawful purposes. This
+License does not grant rights to third-party content, trademarks, publicity rights or personal
+data.
+
+## 3. The DRM-Free Promise
+
+1. **Licensor distributes the Covered Game DRM-free.** A build Licensor releases under AGL-1.1
+   requires no activation server, online check, account binding, install limit, mandatory client,
+   launcher or client-side license enforcement to install or play. A storefront may require an
+   account to download the build; once downloaded, the build does not.
+2. **Do not add DRM.** If You distribute a mod, patch or code change permitted by Section 2.5, You
+   must not add digital rights management, activation, always-online requirements or play-blocking
+   telemetry. This is a condition of the license, not a request.
+3. **No anti-consumer technical measures.** You must not use anti-cheat, kernel drivers or account
+   bans to prevent the personal play, modding, backup, recording or streaming Section 2 allows.
+   Protecting a legitimate multiplayer service from cheating is permitted; using anti-cheat to
+   disable single-player rights is not.
+4. **No circumvention.** AL-1.1 Section 5.7 (no circumvention of technical measures) still applies.
+   Under AGL-1.1 there should be no such measure in a Licensor build; if one appears by mistake,
+   report it to `aegean-licensing@proton.me` instead of relying on this section. Good-faith
+   security research on a copy You are licensed to use, and coordinated disclosure, are permitted;
+   this section does not forbid testing Your own copy or reporting a vulnerability.
+5. **Online services may end.** Licensor may change or discontinue online features, servers or
+   accounts. The offline build You already have remains playable to the extent it does not depend
+   on those services, and Licensor will not intentionally break offline play.
+6. **Consumer rights.** Nothing in this License limits mandatory consumer rights in Your country,
+   including refunds, conformity guarantees and the right to use a lawfully acquired copy. A
+   seller's refund policy applies to the sale; this License governs copying and use.
+7. **No compatibility warranty.** A DRM-free build may be copied, but Licensor does not promise
+   that every storefront, console or platform will accept a DRM-free distribution.
+
+## 4. Commercial Use
+
+Commercial or Organizational Use of a Covered Game is not licensed under AGL-1.1. It needs the
+Aegean Commercial License 1.0 (ACL-1.0), evidenced by a certificate that names the project.
+Because ACL-1.0 covers software code and logic, Game Assets also need a separate written asset
+grant under AegeanWorks' `ASSET_POLICY.md`. Commercial use includes, without limitation:
+
+1. selling, renting or licensing the game, a sequel, a remaster or a derivative game;
+2. paid mods, paid asset packs, soundtracks, art books, subscriptions, battle passes,
+   advertising-supported builds and in-game purchases;
+3. publishing on a storefront under a company, employer or client, or bundling the game with a
+   commercial product;
+4. operating a paid, public or managed game server whose main value is the Covered Game; and
+5. using the game or its assets to train, fine-tune, evaluate or benchmark an AI model, which
+   AL-1.1 Section 5.6 already reserves to a commercial license with the `ai-training` Allowance.
+
+Streaming, recording and free fan works under Section 2 are not Commercial Use of the game.
+
+## 5. Versions and Restrictions
+
+1. AL-1.1 Section 10 applies: each Version is licensed separately, and a later Version may be
+   released under different terms, including all rights reserved. The DRM-free promise applies to
+   a Version Licensor actually released under AGL-1.1; a later commercial Version may differ.
+2. You may not remove the `Required Notice:` line, this License or any Asset Manifest from a copy
+   You keep or share as Section 2 allows.
+3. Independent games and tools You create without Covered Material remain Yours. A game that
+   embeds or derives from Covered Material is governed by Section 4.
+
+---
+
+**End of the Aegean Game License 1.1 (AGL-1.1)**
